AI 資本支出牽動股市，債市關注 Fed 政策走向
2026 年 11 月
10 月全球主要股市普遍回落，主要受累於 AI 資本支出攀升與融資壓力引發科技股評價修正。以
美元計價的報酬來看，MSCI 新興亞洲下跌 8.6%最弱，新興市場跌 8.1%；其中韓國 KOSPI 單月重
挫 16.2%、台股跌 5.7%，反映市場重新評估 AI 投資回收效益，半導體股首當其衝。MSCI 拉丁美洲
則下跌 7.7%，因巴西總統魯拉連任後財政紀律疑慮升高。另一方面，MSCI 印度上漲 1.2%表現最佳，
受企業獲利與資本利得稅調降預期支撐。歐股則受惠於資金轉向醫療保健等防禦性類股，加上能源股

受油價上漲帶動，抵銷了銀行類股的跌勢，惟歐元貶值也壓縮了其以美元計價的表現。
聯準會 10 月雖維持利率不變，但全球債市轉由避險需求主導，AI 投資融資及信用疑慮促使資金
流向公債，美國 10 年期公債殖利率由 9 月底 5.29%降至 10 月底的 4.94%。全球主權債上漲 1.34%
居冠，新興美元債與全球投資級公司債分別上漲 1.26%、1.18%。惟信用利差擴大抵銷部分利率紅利，
聖路易 Fed 資料顯示，美國高收益債利差(ICE BofA US High Yield Index OAS)由 9 月底的 312 bps
升至 10/29 的 326bps，導致全球高收益債僅漲 0.50%，亞洲高收益債更下跌 0.95%。新興當地債
10 月下跌 0.64%，主要受到巴西選後財政疑慮及匯率波動拖累。
在股市方面，儘管 10 月科技股因 AI 資本支出與投資回收疑慮出現明顯修正，但全球企業獲利表
現仍具韌性。隨著 AI 基礎建設持續擴張，HBM、伺服器及先進製程等相關供應鏈仍具中長期成長動

能；然而，市場關注焦點已由資本支出規模轉向投資效益與自由現金流，企業獲利能否符合高度預期，
將成為影響股價表現的關鍵。整體而言，美國、台灣及日本等具備 AI 供應鏈與科技產業優勢的市場
仍值得關注，惟 11 月美國期中選舉結果、聯準會政策預期及能源價格變化，仍可能牽動市場風險偏
好，預期後續股市將呈現獲利基本面主導、產業與個股表現分化的格局。
就債市而言，10 月下旬避險買盤帶動美國 10 年期公債殖利率回落至約 4.94%，但聯準會政策方
向仍偏緊。10 月 FOMC 以 10 比 2 維持利率於 3.75%～4.00%，兩名反對者均主張升息 1 碼，市場
對 12 月升息的預期機率已逾七成，加上多位官員釋出鷹派訊號，短期殖利率仍有反彈風險。另一方
面，美國經濟成長具韌性，財政赤字及公債供給壓力亦可能限制長天期殖利率下行。投資級債方面，
雖具票息收益優勢，但 AI 企業融資與新債供給增加，仍可能使信用利差承壓。整體而言，中天期美
國公債及信用品質良好的投資級公司債，仍具中期收益吸引力，惟升息預期尚未消退，應審慎控管存

續期間風險，並留意通膨、聯準會政策及信用利差變化等因素。

美國股市：獲利成長支撐基本面，AI 投資回收與高利率牽動評價
10 月美股走勢受到大型科技股評價、通膨預期及企業財報交互影響，標普 500 指數在月內呈現
震盪，指數領漲產業亦輪動。月初 AI 需求支撐大型科技股；中旬隨能源價格上揚及美債殖利率波動，
部分高本益比股票面臨獲利了結。月底大型科技公司公布財報後，市場對高資本支出的疑慮升溫，科
技股賣壓擴大。10 月 26 日至 30 日標普 500 下跌 0.57%，其中通訊服務與資訊科技分別下跌 5.32%
及 2.03%，醫療保健、原物料及公用事業則分別上漲 3.12%、2.79%及 2.04%。資金由科技權值股

轉向防禦性及傳統產業，指數內部表現分化。
美國總經方面，10 月 S&P Global 綜合 PMI 初值維持擴張，企業活動具韌性，但房市等利率敏
感產業承壓。能源價格走高使通膨回落存在不確定性。聯準會於 10 月 28 日 FOMC 會議決議維持聯
邦資金利率於 3.75%至 4.00%，符合市場預期，並將經濟活動評估上修為強勁；兩名官員則支持升
息一碼，突顯決策層對物價壓力的看法仍有差異。主席於記者會指出，先前政策緊縮已逐步影響家庭
及企業的信用條件，資本配置也趨向審慎。會後升息預期與美債殖利率波動，牽動科技股評價。就業、
薪資及通膨走勢仍是政策關鍵。
2026 年第三季美股企業財報整體維持成長，資訊科技與能源為獲利預估上修的主要來源。10 月
初研究顯示，資訊科技產業第三季每股盈餘預估上修 3.5%，能源產業上修 18%，但原物料與部分消
費類股遭下調，產業間獲利趨勢並不一致。不過，市場對企業獲利的檢視逐步由營收及訂單成長，延
伸至設備折舊、資本支出、融資負擔及自由現金流。部分公司即使業績優於預期，仍因財測保守或現

金流偏弱而承壓。大型科技股價反應取決於資本支出與獲利轉換效率。相較過去單純以營收成長衡量
科技需求，市場開始檢視折舊負擔對淨利率的影響。
展望 11 月，美股企業獲利仍可提供中期支撐，但利率維持高檔、AI 相關評價偏高，以及政策不
確定性，可能使主要指數短期波動加劇。產業配置維持大型科技核心部位，優先考量具技術壁壘、穩
定客戶需求及自由現金流的企業；對資本支出增幅明顯高於營收成長、融資需求較高或估值已充分反
映成長預期的個股，則宜採取較保守的部位管理。半導體與雲端供應鏈仍須區分訂單能見度與投資回
收期。醫療保健及公用事業需求較穩，可能吸引防禦性資金。後續變數包括科技企業財測、通膨、就
業與期中選舉後政策方向。

歐洲股市：法國財政與能源風險升溫，留意殖利率對經濟與股市之影響
10 月泛歐指數震盪收高，較 9 月底 634.89 點上漲約 1.87%，為 6 月以來最佳單月表現。月初受
到油價攀升、歐洲公債殖利率走高及法國財政疑慮干擾，指數承壓，銀行與利率敏感類股表現偏弱；
隨後企業財報陸續公布，能源與國防相關企業獲利支撐市場反彈，醫療保健等防禦性產業亦受到青
睞，部分藥廠上調營運展望後股價走強。下旬指數一度升抵逾一個月高點，惟月底美國科技企業財報
引發 AI 投資回收疑慮，歐洲半導體設備與科技股跟隨下挫。10 月漲勢呈現產業輪動與個股財報分化。

歐元區經濟維持擴張但動能趨緩。第三季 GDP 初值季增 0.4%，低於第二季的 0.6%，年增 1.3%；
10 月綜合 PMI 初值由 9 月 53.1 降至 51.9，反映企業新訂單成長放慢，法國罷工、政治動盪及融資
成本上升亦壓抑商業活動。能源價格推升投入成本，企業轉嫁能力卻未同步增強，形成成長放緩與物
價壓力並存的局面，企業招聘步調也趨於保守。ECB 於 10 月 29 日維持政策利率不變，指出能源衝
擊可能同時提高通膨並抑制成長，且融資環境已較前次會議緊縮。總裁拉加德表示曾討論立即升息，
將等待更多數據，而法國公債風險溢酬升高亦可能限制政策操作空間。
企業獲利則是 10 月歐股表現的重要支撐。依 LSEG 截至 10 月 29 日彙整的 144 家已公布財報企
業及其餘公司預估，STOXX 600 第三季獲利年增率上修至 23.6%，接近第二季 23.9%的成長幅度，
營收預估年增 13.6%。能源類股受油價上揚與煉油利差改善帶動，獲利預估年增 139%，為整體成長
主要來源，且明顯高於其他產業；扣除能源後，其餘企業獲利成長約 9.8%，仍高於過去 16 季平均

7.1%。產業分化持續明顯，國防與部分科技設備商受惠訂單需求，ASML 與 Ericsson 公布的業績亦
反映設備支出韌性；反觀汽車、零售等非必需消費產業面臨需求疲弱，房地產業受高利率與融資成本
壓抑，市場對財測指引及現金流品質的要求提高。
展望 11 月，歐洲能源股短期受惠高油價及煉油利差，但獲利基期與地緣政治變動可能擴大股價
波動，宜避免將當季高成長率直接推算為全年可望維持相同成長動能；國防、電力設備及具全球競爭
力的工業企業，若訂單與定價能力維持，仍具相對優勢。金融股須區分銀行個別資本體質與法國主權
財政風險，並觀察公債利差變化對融資成本的傳導；房地產及高度依賴歐洲內需的循環性消費產業則
偏審慎。後續關注 ECB 十二月利率決策、能源通膨走勢、法國預算協商及企業財測修正，若殖利率
再度上升而獲利改善未能擴散，歐股評價上行空間將受限制，個股分化可能延續。

日本股市：日銀暫緩升息但確認物價達標，半導體回檔後聚焦財報與 12 月政策
10 月日本股市的觀察主軸，集中於日本銀行政策動向、通膨變化與 AI 投資帶動的出口成長。日
銀於 10 月 30 日維持政策利率 1.25%不變，惟投票比由 1 票支持升息、其餘委員主張維持利率，轉
為 5 比 4，顯示內部對進一步緊縮的支持增加。會後市場對升息時點的預期並未明顯改變，焦點仍集
中於 12 月。日銀展望報告指出，通膨大致符合 2%目標，並將 2027 財年核心 CPI 預估由 2.4%上修
至 2.6%。此外，ETF 年出售規模由 3,300 億日圓調降至 2,860 億日圓，反映政策正常化過程仍兼顧

市場承受能力，整體呈現利率暫停調整、緊縮基調延續的格局。
基本面方面，日本 9 月出口年增 16.7%，雖低於 8 月的 19.3%，仍連續第 13 個月成長，顯示外
需維持韌性。半導體等電子零組件、汽車及半導體製造設備出口，分別成長 49.4%、15.1%及 43.9%，
其中 AI 相關需求成為重要支柱。依出口地區觀察，對美出口年增 22.9%，半導體製造設備更大增
93.1%；對亞洲出口成長 17.5%，亦提供主要支撐。相較之下，對中國出口增幅放緩至 8.6%，主因
汽車出口年減 59.8%，顯示不同市場與產業的表現仍有落差。整體而言，出口成長並非全面同步擴張，
而是由 AI 投資與半導體設備需求帶動，後續仍須觀察成長動能能否延伸至更多產業。
展望 11 月，日本股市可聚焦通膨走勢、日銀政策訊號，以及 AI 資本支出的延續性。9 月 CPI 年
增率由 1.9%升至 2.1%，若加回能源政策約 0.82 個百分點的壓抑效果，實質漲幅達 2.9%，加上食品
與外食價格仍有上漲壓力，支持日銀維持偏緊縮立場。若後續數據持續反映物價壓力，12 月升息預

期可能成為影響市場評價的重要因素。產業方面，全球 AI 基礎建設與半導體資本支出若持續擴張，
日本半導體設備及電子零組件出口可望獲得支撐；韓國 10 月上旬半導體出口大幅成長，也提供區域
需求的觀察線索。不過，對中出口放緩及汽車需求變化，仍是評估外需時不可忽略的風險。
整體而言，AI 投資熱潮與出口成長韌性，為日本股市後續表現帶來正向契機。儘管日銀政策正常
化仍是市場關注焦點，但全球 AI 基礎建設持續推進，有望支撐日本半導體設備及電子零組件需求，
並為相關企業帶來營運成長機會。可持續聚焦出口擴張與科技投資所帶動的基本面改善。若需求動能
延續並轉化為獲利成長，日本股市可望在政策調整過程中，持續展現結構性成長題材的吸引力。

台灣股市：外銷訂單連四月創高，台積電上修展望，市場料更重視獲利驗證
10 月台股基本面持續交出亮眼成績。經濟部公布，9 月外銷訂單達 1,142.1 億美元，月增 10.9%、
年增 62.4%，連續第四個月創單月新高，且已連續兩個月突破千億美元；第三季外銷訂單 3,151.0 億
美元，年增 65.0%，累計前三季 8,192 億美元，已超越去年全年的 7,437 億美元。貨品別方面，資
訊通信與電子產品分別達 438.8 億及 474.2 億美元，年增 87.3%及 71.0%，皆創單月新高；地區別
方面，美國訂單 453.8 億美元、年增 79.4%，主要來自伺服器、手機及筆電。企業端，台積電第三

季營收 467.2 億美元，季增 16.2%並優於財測高標，第四季營收預估 523 億至 543 億美元，全年美
元營收成長上修至約 44%，顯示 AI 需求仍未降溫。
對照廠商對 9 月接單的預期與實際結果，可發現 AI 需求向供應鏈擴散的判斷大致獲得驗證。當
時資訊通信與電子產品的訂單金額動向指數分別為 52.4 及 51.5，實際接單即雙雙創下單月新高；基
本金屬與化學品的動向指數高達 59.0 及 61.0，9 月接單亦分別年增 29.7%及 22.8%，其中基本金屬
受惠 AI 供應鏈帶動銅箔、銅箔基板需求增加。機械指數雖僅 46.3，但在半導體業者擴大資本支出帶
動下，9 月接單仍年增 21.5%並創同月新高。與 AI 供應鏈相關的傳統貨品表現較佳，但部分傳產仍
面臨供給過剩壓力，產業並非全面同步復甦。
展望 11 月，台股將在強勁基本面與外部評價調整之間拉鋸。10 月南韓股市因記憶體競爭與 AI
投資疑慮重挫，台股相對抗跌，加權指數月中一度再創歷史新高，但 10/15 及 10/30 仍分別出現 3.41%

與 2.49%的單日跌幅，顯示外資對 AI 題材的獲利了結賣壓仍在。經濟部預估 10 月外銷訂單將介於
1,125 億至 1,159 億美元，年增 62%至 67%，按金額計算的動向指數為 50.5，若高於 9 月的 1,142.1
億美元，將連續第五個月創單月新高。台積電亦重申 2026 年資本支出 600 億至 640 億美元，並表
示 2026 至 2028 年支出將顯著高於前三年，設備、廠務及先進封裝供應鏈的訂單能見度仍高。不過，
台積電也提醒消費性與價格敏感市場正面臨零組件漲價與總經不確定性，對 2027 年規劃採審慎態度。
整體而言，11 月維持偏正面的台股觀點，以重視獲利驗證作為配置方向。核心部位可聚焦晶圓
代工、先進封裝、伺服器代工及 ASIC 等需求明確且具定價能力的企業；擴散機會則可從銅箔基板、
高階材料、設備與廠務工程中，篩選已取得實際訂單、營收與毛利率同步成長的公司。對於評價已高、
主要依賴題材想像的個股，宜適度觀察，避免在市場重新檢視 AI 投入回收時承受較大波動。留意 11

月中下旬公布的 10 月外銷訂單、美國大型科技業者資本支出指引及輝達財報對市場情緒的影響。

中國股市：外需撐起生產，內需與房市仍疲弱，政策加碼成為關鍵
中國第三季經濟表現略優於預期。國家統計局公布，第三季 GDP 年增 4.5%，高於第二季的 4.3%，
前三季累計成長 4.6%，仍落在政府全年 4.5%至 5.0%的目標區間內。成長動能主要來自供給端與外
需：第三季製造業增加值年增 5.9%，資訊傳輸、軟體與資訊技術服務業年增 9.8%；前三季貨物進出
口年增 18.8%，受惠全球 AI 開發加速與電動車需求擴大，高科技產品出口表現強勁。另一方面，受
中東情勢推升原油與原物料價格影響，9 月 PPI 年增 5.0%，為今年以來最大漲幅，上游資源類企業

的獲利有望改善，但中下游製造業則面臨成本轉嫁壓力。
然而，從圖中可見，生產面與需求面的落差仍大。9 月工業生產年增 5.5%，服務業生產指數年
增 4.8%，兩者維持溫和成長；但房地產仍是經濟最大拖累，9 月房地產投資年減 26.2%，跌幅較 8
月的 25.4%進一步擴大。銷售端同步轉弱，銷售面積與銷售額分別年減 20.5%及 20.0%，較 8 月的
14.7%及 12.1%明顯惡化；竣工面積跌幅更由 28.4%擴大至 36.4%。新開工跌幅雖由 31.0%收斂至
25.6%，在建面積則持平於年減 12.8%，顯示開發商仍以去化庫存、控制投資為主，房市尚未出現止
穩訊號，並持續壓抑家庭財富效果與地方政府的土地財政收入。
內需疲弱是當前中國經濟的核心課題。9 月社會消費品零售總額僅年增 1.1%，汽車與家電等耐
用品銷售停滯，以舊換新補貼效果逐漸遞減；前三季固定資產投資年減 7.2%，基建與工業投資同步
下滑，第三季建築業增加值亦年減 4.4%。統計局也坦言國內需求不足的問題仍然突出。政策方面，7

月中央政治局會議定調下半年實施更積極的財政政策與適度寬鬆的貨幣政策，並適時推出增量政策；
10 月下旬召開的二十屆五中全會則以黨建議題為主。市場表現上，10/30 上證綜合指數受半導體股
拖累下跌 1.44%，香港恒生指數則在必需消費與能源股帶動下上漲 1.46%，資金明顯偏好防禦型內
需與高股息族群。
整體而言，對中國股市宜維持中性偏審慎的態度。經濟成長雖守住目標區間，但房地產投資與銷
售跌幅擴大、零售成長放緩，內需尚未出現明確回升訊號，在增量刺激政策落地前，指數大幅走高的
條件仍不充分。配置上可聚焦兩條主線：一是受惠出口與產業升級的高端製造、資訊科技服務與國產
算力供應鏈，但須留意資本支出擴張對現金流的壓力；二是具穩定配息與現金流的高股息、公用事業
及必需消費族群，在內需疲弱時提供防禦。後續可觀察年底中央經濟工作會議對財政擴張與房市支持
的力度，作為調整布局的依據。

拉美市場：魯拉意外連任引發財政紀律疑慮，巴西股市重挫
10 月拉美市場走勢主要受巴西總統大選左右。月初弗拉維奧・波索納洛意外在首輪總統選舉中
領先，提高市場對政黨輪替與財政改革的期待，帶動巴西股市同步走強；但 10 月 25 日現任總統
魯拉在決選中仍以 51.79%的得票率勝出，市場原先的期待落空，轉而擔憂巴西財政赤字可能進一步
惡化，巴西資產因此遭到拋售。由於巴西約占 MSCI 拉丁美洲指數約六成權重，巴西股市下跌使拉
美市場整體出現明顯回落，後續巴西股市能否回穩，仍要觀察魯拉新任期提出的具體財政政策。
市場之所以對魯拉的財政政策如此敏感，主要是因為巴西的債務負擔仍在持續增加。高盛預估，

巴西一般政府總債務占國內生產毛額的比率，將由 2025 年的 78.6%升至 2026 年底的 83.5%。然
而，巴西部分支出不易削減，既有稅負又偏高，使政府調整收支的空間相當有限。在此情況下，高盛
預期魯拉新任期可能僅能溫和抑制不含利息的政府支出成長，並將依賴增加稅收來改善收支，推動大
幅結構性改革的可能性則相對較低。若巴西財政調整最終以增稅為主，而支出未能有效控制，市場不
僅會擔心巴西財政赤字難以縮小，也會擔心新增稅額侵蝕企業獲利。
此外，即使魯拉政府提出新的財政改革方案，仍可能受制於政治立場相左的新任國會。在本次國
會改選後，右派與中間偏右政黨合計將掌握眾議院約 59%、參議院約 64%的席次，弗拉維奧所屬的
自由黨也成為參眾兩院的最大黨，因此魯拉推動預算與改革時，必須爭取政治立場不同的議員支持。
高盛報告指出，儘管巴西國會中間政黨仍可能與魯拉政府合作，但可能以內閣職位、預算分配及國營
企業人事作為交換條件。這樣的協商雖有助魯拉政府在國會取得多數支持，卻也可能使原有方案遭到
修改，進而影響巴西財政整頓的幅度與速度。

巴西財政政策的變化，最終會透過利率與匯率影響股市。巴西現行 Selic 政策利率為 13.75%，
選前市場原本預期巴西央行將延續降息步調，但選後巴西里爾急貶，進口成本上升恐進一步推升通
膨，限制巴西央行降息空間，在利率條件緊縮的狀況下，也將使仰賴國內需求的企業獲利受到壓抑。
不過，儘管巴西財政政策仍有變數，巴西經濟本身仍具備一定的支撐條件。高盛報告指出，巴西
農業具國際競爭力，油氣產量持續成長，並在綠色能源與關鍵礦物領域具有發展潛力；此外，巴西的
貿易順差、外國直接投資與充裕外匯存底，也能為外部資金波動提供緩衝，持續為巴西經濟提供支撐。
整體而言，巴西的產業優勢與外部收支條件，使巴西經濟基本面仍具支撐。然而，魯拉新任期的
財政部長人選、2027 年預算安排，以及魯拉政府與國會的協商進展，將左右市場對巴西財政與降息
的預期，在財政政策方向明朗之前，短線拉美市場波動恐將加劇。

科技類股：CSP 資本支出續增壓縮現金流，AI 受惠族群走向分化
10 月下旬美國大型科技業者第三季財報，讓市場對 AI 的關注由投入多少轉向何時回收。營運面
普遍不差：Meta 第三季營收 646 億美元、年增 26%，廣告單價年增 14%，顯示 AI 精準投放已轉化
為實際收入；Alphabet 旗下 Google Cloud 營收年增 103%至 307.1 億美元。然而，Alphabet 搜尋
營收成長放緩至 12%，加上自由現金流為負 90.7 億美元、遠差於市場預期，股價於 10/29 單日重挫
12.6%，創上市以來最大單日跌幅；Meta 自由現金流亦轉為負 21.2 億美元。相較之下，微軟資本支

出低於預期、自由現金流維持正值。市場並非否定 AI 需求，而是開始以現金流紀律區分企業。
從四大雲端服務業者（CSP）最新一季的營業現金流與資本支出，可清楚看出壓力所在。Amazon
資本支出約 759 億美元，遠高於約 424 億美元的營業現金流，缺口最大；Alphabet 資本支出約 514
億美元，同樣超過約 423 億美元的營業現金流；Meta 資本支出約 349 億美元，亦略高於約 328 億
美元的營業現金流。四家業者中，僅微軟營業現金流約 580 億美元，明顯高於約 394 億美元的資本
支出。這意味多數 CSP 已需動用既有現金或對外融資支應 AI 建置，投資節奏將更受資金成本與股東
回報要求牽制；但從另一面看，各業者全年資本支出指引並未下修，Meta 與 Alphabet 分別維持 1,350
億至 1,450 億美元及 1,950 億至 2,050 億美元的區間，對上游供應鏈而言，短期訂單能見度仍高。
依 AI 需求能見度與財務韌性兩個面向劃分科技子產業版圖來看。核心受惠區的大型 CSP、半導

體設備、CPU／ASIC 及高階光通訊，兼具明確訂單與穩健財務，例如 Lumentum 最新一季營收年增
109%、非 GAAP 毛利率突破 50%，並指出部分雷射元件供給仍吃緊。穩健型的消費電子、手機晶
片與類比 IC，AI 需求能見度較低，但財務結構穩定，可在科技股波動時發揮防禦作用；伺服器整機
與組裝雖受惠出貨成長，卻面臨毛利偏薄、零組件成本與能源成本上升的風險，股價波動較大。至於
高槓桿的 AI 算力出租業者，在利率居高與信用利差擴大下最為脆弱，10 月 CoreWeave 債券價格下
跌即引發市場對 AI 信用風險的擔憂，成為本波修正的導火線之一。
整體而言，科技股以「核心基本面受惠為主、穩健型為輔」來觀察。可聚焦半導體設備、CPU
／ASIC 及高階光通訊等兼具訂單能見度與財務韌性的族群，並以 11 月指標性廠商 Lumentum、輝
達等財報驗證需求延續性；大型 CSP 中，則可優先選擇營業現金流足以支應資本支出、投資節奏較
具紀律的業者。穩健型的消費電子與手機晶片等，可作為降低整體波動的衛星配置。伺服器整機與組

裝族群宜觀察具議價能力者，避免在零組件與能源成本上升時承受毛利壓力。AI 長期趨勢未變，但
市場已進入用現金流與業績說話的階段，選股應更重視需求能否轉化為獲利，對科技股維持正向看法。

能源類股：柴油短缺支撐油價，但若中東衝突緩解，明年原油有望轉為供過於求
10 月西德州原油價格隨荷莫茲海峽油輪與沙烏地阿拉伯能源設施持續遇襲，整體走勢仍呈現震
盪走高。展望 11 月，偏低的能源庫存與冬季需求預期仍有利油價支撐；然而，國際能源署（IEA）、
美國能源資訊署（EIA）與石油輸出國組織（OPEC）等 3 大能源機構於 10 月月報同步下修今年石
油需求預估，且 IEA 與 EIA 均預期明年供給增幅將遠高於需求，故若後續中東局勢出現好轉，石油
供應緊張情況也將有望逐步緩解。
3 大能源機構在 10 月月報均下修今年需求預估，IEA 將 2026 年全球石油需求由年減每日

250 萬桶調整為年減 260 萬桶，EIA 由年減約 170 萬桶調整為年減約 200 萬桶，OPEC 則由年
增每日 38 萬桶下修至 18 萬桶，預估分歧主要來自中國、中東等非 OECD 國家的需求預估。
展望明年，IEA 與 EIA 均預期石油市場將轉趨寬鬆。IEA 預估 2027 年全球供給將較今年增加
每日 770 萬桶，需求僅增加 200 萬桶；EIA 則預估供給增加約每日 850 萬桶，需求僅增加約 220
萬桶，顯示若後續中東受阻產能能夠恢復，供應回升速度將快於消費。OPEC 的預估則相對看好油
價，OPEC 將 2027 年需求由 9 月預估的每日增加 236 萬桶上調至增加 255 萬桶，並預估市場
需要 OPEC+ 提供的原油將由今年的每日 4,210 萬桶至明年增至 4,390 萬桶，換言之，OPEC 認
為明年需求成長將超過其他產油國的供給增幅，OPEC+須再增產量才能滿足市場需求。
然而，即使中東衝突後續轉趨緩解，明年原油供需可能轉趨寬鬆，成品油供應仍可能維持緊俏。

IEA 指出，9 月全球柴油出口較去年同期減少約每日 70 萬桶，主因是中東成品油運輸受阻與俄羅
斯煉油設施受損，加上秋季煉油廠進入歲修，使市場難以在入冬前補足柴油庫存。因此，IEA 認為即
使 2027 年第一季全球石油市場恢復供過於求，較明顯的庫存回補仍可能要等到明年下半年。由於
成品油供應偏緊，EIA 雖然下修石油需求預測，卻仍將 2026 年西德州原油均價預測由每桶 84.65
美元上調至 88.21 美元，2027 年則由 69.74 美元上調至 79.74 美元。
另一方面，各國政策措施可望在過渡期間持續提供供應緩衝。G7 於 10 月 2 日承諾在四個月
內協調釋出 1 億桶原油與油品，並優先供應柴油；美國放寬俄羅斯柴油交易限制後，美聯社引述船
運追蹤機構 Kpler 指出，首艘載運約 4 萬噸柴油的油輪已經啟航，預計 11 月 7 日抵達紐約，均
有利於緩解短期成品油短缺問題。整體而言，偏低的成品油庫存與中東航運風險，短期仍有利支撐油
價，然而在三大機構均下修今年石油需求預估，且 IEA 與 EIA 預期明年供給增幅將明顯高於需求的情

況下，後續油價進一步大幅上漲空間也相對有限。

黃金類股：中國及全球央行買盤仍穩健，然短線市場資金轉向流出
近期金價走勢仍未擺脫偏弱格局，主因是中東能源供應風險推升油價，市場擔憂通膨難以迅速降
溫，並持續評估聯準會於今明兩年再度升息，而利率維持高檔將提高持有黃金的機會成本。此外，
10 月中下旬開始部分避險資金轉向美國公債，北美黃金 ETF 也出現資金撤出，使金價即使在美元
與美債殖利率出現回落的情況下，仍缺乏買盤支撐。然而，中長線來看，全球央行購金的趨勢目前
仍未出現明顯轉向，加上中國投資買盤持續流入，均有助支撐中長期黃金需求。
從黃金 ETF 資金來看，黃金投資需求在 10 月下旬由全面流入轉為地區分化。世界黃金協會資

料顯示，9 月全球實物黃金 ETF 淨流入 100 億美元，持有量增加 67 噸至 4,256 噸，創下持有
量歷史新高；第三季累計淨流入 310 億美元，也創下單季新高紀錄，然而，截至 10 月 23 日的
一週，全球黃金 ETF 轉為淨流出約 12 億美元，持有量減少 7.7 噸，其中北美黃金 ETF 淨流出
16.9 億美元，顯示隨金價再度向下破底，ETF 資金也開始轉趨保守。
然而另一方面，中國投資需求仍持續為金價提供支撐。世界黃金協會指出，中國黃金 ETF 9 月
淨流入人民幣 110 億元，持有量增加 11 噸至 304 噸，創下月底新高，且截至 10 月 23 日買
盤仍在延續。世界黃金協會指出中國股市表現偏弱，提高了當地投資人分散資產的需求，而中國公
債殖利率下降，也降低了持有黃金的機會成本，雙雙有利於中國資金流入黃金市場。
此外，央行購金仍是金價的結構性支撐。世界黃金協會統計，全球央行 8 月申報的淨購金量為
39 噸；另中國人民銀行 9 月增持 23 噸，為其連續第 23 個月增持黃金配置，波蘭央行年初至 8

月也累計購入 98 噸，使其總持有量達 648 噸，逐步接近波蘭央行設下的 700 噸目標。另一方面，
由於許多央行購金並未公開申報，高盛以倫敦金庫黃金流出量與英國黃金淨出口之間的差額，推估
未申報的央行買盤，估計全球央行 8 月實際購金量約 79 噸，經季節調整的三個月平均約每月 85
噸，遠高於 2022 年以前平均每月約 17 噸的水準。高盛報告指出，新興市場央行的黃金配置比重
仍低於長期合理水準，加上各國仍有動機分散持有七大工業國（G7）貨幣資產，因此預估在未來央
行買盤仍可望延續。
但仍需注意的是，也有個別央行開始調整黃金配置。哈薩克央行於 10 月中旬表示，由於 10 年
期美債殖利率升至 24 年高點，提高了美元資產的吸引力，故計畫在 2026 年底前，將黃金占儲備
資產的比重由約 78% 降至 75%。以哈薩克央行 386 噸的黃金持有量估算，此次調整規模約相當於
15 噸。雖然哈薩克央行的調整規模不大，但若其他黃金占比偏高的央行也跟進再平衡，全球央行需
求對金價的支撐力道可能因此減弱。

整體而言，全球央行購金趨勢尚未出現明顯轉向，加上中國投資買盤，均有助支撐中長期黃金需
求。然而，現階段高漲的通膨與升息預期，加上短線 ETF 資金撤出，仍可能使短期金價面臨震盪。

### Page 11

任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本

固定收益：避險需求支撐美債，聚焦中期美債及投資級債
10 月美國公債殖利率呈現月初攀升、月中震盪回落、月底再度下行的走勢。月初受能源價格上
漲、通膨預期升溫，以及 AI 投資帶動經濟成長與資金需求增加影響，10 年期美債殖利率於 10 月 5
日升至 5.31%，30 年期升至 5.66%。隨後市場對 AI 基礎建設融資與企業現金流的疑慮升高，科技股
賣壓帶動資金轉向公債，10 年期殖利率於 10 月 15 日降至 5.10%；22 日大型科技股重挫再度推升
避險需求，進一步降至 4.96%。月底雖然美國經濟維持韌性、聯準會於 28 日維持利率不變，通膨及

財政赤字仍限制長天期利率下行空間，但科技企業資本支出疑慮持續支撐債市。
美國經濟呈現就業成長放緩、通膨壓力仍高的局面。9 月非農就業僅增加 2.9 萬人，失業率由 4.1%
升至 4.2%，反映企業聘僱動能趨緩；惟通膨尚未明顯改善，9 月 CPI 年增 3.7%，月增 0.6%，主要
受能源價格上漲帶動，核心 CPI 年增 2.6%。聯準會偏好的核心 PCE 物價指數年增 3.1%，仍高於 2%
目標。Fed 於 10 月 28 日以 10 比 2 票維持政策利率於 3.75%至 4.00%不變，並將經濟活動評估由
「穩健」上調至「強勁」，指出消費支出具韌性、企業資本投資維持強勁，兩位官員則因通膨疑慮主
張升息一碼。Fed 強調將等待更多經濟數據再決定政策方向，未明確承諾後續升息時點，顯示就業放
緩與通膨黏著仍是政策判斷的主要拉鋸因素。
信用市場方面，十月中旬 CoreWeave 公司債價格重挫，凸顯人工智慧基礎建設高度仰賴外部融
資的脆弱環節。根據路透報導，公司債務規模約五百億美元，部分債券殖利率一度攀升至百分之十五

至十六，五年期信用違約交換利差突破一千一百個基點，顯示投資人要求的違約風險補償大幅提高。
疑慮集中於建置成本、設備折舊、客戶收款與債務支出的時間差，以及再融資成本。即使需求強勁，
合約收入仍不等同即時現金流，建置延宕可能加重償債壓力。相關賣壓亦使投資人重新區分大型科技
企業與高度槓桿資料中心營運商的信用體質。此事件尚不代表整體投資級信用惡化。
展望十一月，美債配置建議仍以中期公債與優質美元投資級公司債為核心，兼顧較高票息水準與
政策預期反覆下的存續期間風險。十年期殖利率回落至百分之五附近後，中天期債券仍較超長天期不
易受期限溢酬波動干擾。投資級公司債可優先篩選利息保障倍數充足、現金流穩健及再融資需求可控
的發行人，避免僅憑信用評等忽略產業資本支出周期。高收益債須檢視利差補償，尤其資料中心借款
人的抵押品與客戶集中度。後續關注十一月美國期中選舉、就業與通膨數據、財政部發債規畫及聯準
會十二月會議預期，並追蹤信用利差是否由個別高槓桿企業擴散至整體市場。

Economic Trends

2026 年 11 月 6 日

1/2 
2026 年・冬のボーナス予測 
～前年比＋3.0％と６年連続の増加を予想～ 
 経済調査部 シニアエグゼクティブエコノミスト  新家 義貴 
（℡：050-5474-7490）

○ 民間企業の2026 年冬のボーナス一人当たり支給額を前年比＋3.0％（昨冬同＋2.8％）と予想する
（厚生労働省「毎月勤労統計」ベース）。冬のボーナスとしては６年連続の増加となるだろう。中
東情勢の悪化に伴う資源価格上昇が企業活動の重荷となるなかでも企業収益は高水準を維持してい
る。人手不足を背景とした処遇改善の動きも根強く、今冬もボーナスの増加が続くと予想する。

○ 11 月６日に厚生労働省から公表された26 年夏のボーナス（賞与支給事業所における労働者一人平
均賞与額）は、前年比＋5.3％と５年連続で増加し、昨夏の同＋2.9％から伸びも拡大した。また、
賞与を支給していない事業所も含む「全事業所における労働者一人平均賞与額」で見ても同＋
5.9％と増加している。賞与を支給する事業所の割合も前年の 73.4％から74.0％へ上昇しており、
企業の従業員への還元姿勢が強いことがうかがえる。

○ ボーナスの交渉では、春闘時にその年の年間賞与を決定する夏冬型が採用されていることが多い。
2026 年春闘では、月例給与だけでなく一時金でも増額回答が目立った。企業収益が高水準にある
ことに加え、物価高への配慮や人材確保の観点から、賞与を含めた処遇改善を進める企業が多かっ
たとみられる。また、月例給与の引き上げが賞与の算定基礎額を押し上げ、支給月数の増加と相ま
ってボーナス増につながる面もある。夏のボーナス増に続き、冬についてもこうした交渉結果が反
映される形で増加が予想される。

○ 毎期型の企業、あるいは労働組合が存在しない中小・零細企業においては、より直近の収益状況・
業況がボーナスに反映されやすい。もっとも、イラン情勢悪化によるコスト上昇が企業収益の下押
し要因となるなかでも、価格転嫁の進展などを背景として企業収益は総じて底堅く推移している。
利益の水準も高く、従業員への還元余力はなお保たれているとみてよいだろう。

○ 人手不足感が強いことも、ボーナス増加を後押しする。企業にとって人材の確保・定着は引き続き
重要な経営課題となっており、月例給与だけでなく賞与を含めた処遇改善の必要性は高い。物価高
によって家計負担が増加するなか、従業員への還元を求める声も強い。こうした点も今冬のボーナ
ス増加を支える要因となる。

○ 労務行政研究所が９月30 日に公表した調査によれば、東証プライム上場企業 175 社の 2026 年年末

一時金妥結額は、前年比＋4.2％と５年連続で増加した。製造業が同＋4.3％、非製造業が同＋
3.5％と、いずれもプラスとなっている。大企業中心の調査であり、中小企業やパートタイム労働
者も含む毎月勤労統計とは対象が異なるものの、冬のボーナスも堅調に増加するという見方を裏付

○ 冬のボーナス増加は家計の所得環境にとって好材料であり、個人消費を下支えすることが期待され
る。一方で懸念されるのが物価上昇の影響である。中東情勢悪化に伴うコスト上昇の価格転嫁など
を背景として、今後は物価上昇率が高まる可能性が高く、政府による電気・ガス代支援の効果を織
り込んでも、2027 年初にはＣＰＩコアの前年比上昇率が＋３％台に達することが予想される。物
価上昇による家計の購買力の圧迫が続くなか、ボーナス増加による個人消費の押し上げ効果につい
ては過度な期待は禁物だろう。

more traditional packaging services.  Given recent rising concern of AI investment slowing down, if any, the
management indicates a material slowdown in advanced packaging utilization would affect operating leverage, but

current demand exceeds ASE’s capacity by enough that displaced volume should be replaceable.
• Capex, capacity and principal risks. ASE expects approximately US$11.5bn of capex in 2026, comprising around
US$7bn for machinery and US$4.5bn for facilities and land. It is progressing 22 construction projects, with physical
space representing the most important bottleneck. Management expects 2027 capex to exceed 2026 because
investment is required across full-process packaging, wafer-to-substrate services, probing and testing. Debt

remains the preferred external funding source, supplemented by customer deposits and prepayments. Taiwan will
remain the center for high-end manufacturing, while some trailing-edge capacity is moving to Malaysia and
Singapore to provide geographic diversification and release constrained Taiwanese space.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

3
ASE Technology Holding
Valuation
Our target price of NT$908 is based on 25x 2027 EPS average. Our PER is higher than its five-year range as we expect ASEH to
see accelerating earnings growth in the coming years with GM at upward expansion trend, mainly driven by its promising
outlook in LEAP business and strong expansion for long-term AI demand. At our target price, the shares would trade at a

2026/27E P/B of 10x/8x.
Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent ASE shares from reaching our target price include: 1) poor efficiency and less synergy
created from merger with SPIL; 2) failure to gain market share in advanced packaging; 3) industry oversupply due to aggressive
capacity expansion; 4) severe pricing erosion due to competition; and 5) a longer-than-expected semiconductor inventory
correction.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in

published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is
moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day

period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be
different from and does not affect a stock’s fundamental equity rating, which reflects a longer-term total absolute return

### Page 3

King Yuan Electronics Co (2449.TW)

King Yuan Electronics Co
Valuation
Our target price for KYEC of NT$286 is derived using a DCF methodology. Using Bloomberg data, we apply a risk-free rate of
4%, a risk premium of 5%, and a beta of 1.4x for a 10.8% cost of equity. With 20% corporate tax and a 3.0% interest rate on its
debt, our WACC for KYEC is 9.7%. We assume terminal sales growth of 5%. We model its adjusted EBIT margin to gradually

improve from 23% in 2024 to c30% in 2035E. At our target price, the shares would trade at 2027/28E P/B of 4x/4x and
2027/28E P/E of 25x/17x, in line with its global peers.
Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent KYEC shares from reaching our target price include: 1) weaker-than-expected end-
demand for global smartphone/consumer electronic products; 2) slower ramp for AI-related products; 3) slower-than-
expected demand recovery in the automotive and base station sector; and 4) pricing pressure on increasing competition.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

台新證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市仁愛路四段 118 號 16 樓(02)5589-9558  (113)金管投顧新字第 021 號 
公司拜訪快報

雙鴻(3324)：3Q26 財報不如預期，營運逐季向上 
建議：維持買進

一、雙鴻為散熱解決方案供應商  雙鴻成立於 1998 年 8 月，為國內前二大 NB 散熱模組廠，過去主要生產散熱
器、散熱片、散熱模組、散熱導管、風扇等，現在已轉型為提供客戶完整散熱解
決方案供應商。主要應用於筆記型電腦、桌上型電腦、智慧型手機及伺服器等領
域。3Q26 產品應用領域 Server 81%、PC 14%、VGA/Gaming 4%、及 Others(手
機及車用) 1%。另外 3Q26 水冷業務比重 62%(1H26 約為 55~60%)。

二、3Q26 毛利率 25.65%，低於
預期，3Q26 EPS 11.59 元 
 雙鴻 3Q26 EPS 11.59 元，低於台新預估的 14.13 元，獲利達成率 82%。本業
來看，3Q26 營收 97.87 億元，+12.5%QOQ，+64.3%YOY，低於市場預期之
+15~20%QOQ，七月營收有遞延認列，實際拉貨力道未如預期顯著，3Q26 毛
利率 25.65%，較 2Q26 再衰退 2.77 個百分點，亦低於台新預估的 30%，主要
是報廢品打消，預期 4Q26 毛利率回升，3Q26 EPS 11.59 元。雙鴻 10 月營收
33.72 億元，+17%MOM，+45%YOY，獲利達成率 31%，符合預期，預期 11 月
營收及 12 月營收將優於 10 月，預估 4Q26 營收 110.37 億元，+12.8%QOQ，
+45.6%YOY，預估 4Q26 毛利率 28%，預估 4Q26 EPS 14.79 元。

三、營運逐季成長，氣冷及水冷
兩塊業務均樂觀 
 2025 年雙鴻水冷營收比重 35%，1Q26 提升至 55%，3Q26 提升至 62%，預期
2027 年將提升至 80%，帶動公司業績大幅增長。氣冷部分，2024 年月營收 2-
2.5 億元，2025 年 4～4.5 億元，2026 年平均 6～7 億元，氣冷水冷均樂觀，以
AWS TR3 來看，氣冷及水冷比重至少是 70~80%對比 20~30%，TR4 將為 100%
全水冷，水冷大趨勢不變，VR 9 月起量，VR 營收逐季增，TR3 9 月起量 Compute 
tray 及 Switch tray 及 Rack manifolds 都有相關產品出貨，預計 12 月單月 ASIC
營收比重有機會來到 15~25%，ASIC 水冷產值高於GPU 客戶，其中 Mata>AWS，
2027 年 ASIC 增長可期，公司預期 2027 年 ASIC 營收將超過 GPU。2026 年可
視為 CDU 元年，2027 年營收跳增可期。台新預估 4Q26 營收 110.37 億元，
BVPS 11/18 收盤價 2027 PER 建議

高毛利產品合計來到 59%，此外營運槓桿帶來的效益使本業獲利
率創歷史新高。Interplex 已完整併表，單月貢獻金額來到 6700
萬美金，除了擴大產品線外，合併後同樣有助於加深既有客戶的合
作關係。展望 2027 年，HPC 事業將迎來產品價量齊揚，尤其以高
毛利的 Power 產品最為明顯，根據客戶規劃，1.6T AEC 也將逐步
起量，因此可預期 HPC 占比將會再進一步提升，利於整體營運表
現。綜合以上，研究部預估貿聯 2026 年 EPS 76.23 元，2027 年
EPS 131.41 元，目前 PE 為 11.6x，參考歷史 PE 區間為 10-35x，
考量貿聯獲利將維持高成長期，唯獨近期市場擔憂資料中心建置速
度恐受高利率環境而放緩，存在一定 AI 伺服器延宕部屬之風險，
因此研究部下修 PER 至 22x，目標價 2900 元，維持買進。 
⚫ 公司基本資料 ⚫ 公司基本資料 
貿聯-KY(3665)成立於 1996 年，為全球連接線大廠，總部位於美
國矽谷，生產據點則廣泛分布在全球 37 地。產品應用包含數據通
訊、工廠自動化、半導體設備、醫療、車用等，2025 年工業佔 35%、
通訊 42%、車用 11%、電器 11%、其他 1%。銷售主要為美國 20%、
中國 20%、德國 16%、馬來西亞 7%、台灣 3%、其他 34%。

殖利率(%) 0.87 HPC 部門成長 Q+30%，Y+61%，SPE 也延續上半年以來的穩健

桿效益下也反映在營益率創公司歷史新高。

Interplex 正式併表，產品組合有望再度優化 
貿聯 10 月營收 118.5 億元，M+26.9%，Y+80.0%，自 9 月起完成
對 Interplex 的收購，10 月已完整貢獻營收，單月貢獻金額高達
6700 萬美金，約 20 億台幣，由於 Interplex 既有客戶與貿聯本身
有所重疊，然而產品項目卻有所不同，預計在透過集團整合能力下
將進一步推動公司在客戶群內滲透率的提升。此外貿聯本身新規格
的 Power 產品也持續提高出貨，研究部預期產品組合有望再度優
化，進一步推升獲利率表現。綜合以上，研究部預估貿聯 26Q4 營
收 349.1 億元，Q+25.3%，Y+76.5%，毛利率 33.84%，稅後淨利
56.7 億元，Q+32.0%，Y+106.8%，EPS 28.39 元；2026 年營收
1069.3 億元，Y+50.1%，毛利率 31.83%，稅後淨利 152.1 億元，
Y+69.0%，EPS 76.23 元。

2027 年 HPC 產品維持價量齊揚，光通訊產品 2028 年有望發酵 
隨著 Vera Rubin 進入放量，Rack Busbar 升級至 5000A，Power 
Whip 規格從 60A 升級至 100A 且用量增加，1.6T AEC 也將逐步
開始貢獻營收，另外 Interplex 的加入所帶來的產品優化和客戶關
係的綁定，研究部認為貿聯 HPC 事業仍將迎來顯著的價量齊揚增
長。光傳輸領域上貿聯也持續耕耘，除了新富生 MPO 相關產品，
OCP 上與 ams Osram 的合作也意味著公司已著手布局下一代
Scale up 的通訊方案，研究部看好 wide-and-slow 架構在未來的導
入機會以及市場，預計 2028 年後的發酵有望帶來營運成長動能。
綜合以上，研究部預估貿聯 2027 年營收 1608.0 億元，Y+50.4%，

毛利率 34.14%，稅後淨利 262.3 億元，Y+72.4%，EPS 131.41
元。

結論 
高毛利產品合計來到 59%，此外營運槓桿帶來的效益使本業獲利率創歷
史新高。Interplex 已完整併表，單月貢獻金額來到 6700 萬美金，除了
擴大產品線外，合併後同樣有助於加深既有客戶的合作關係。展望 2027
年，HPC 事業將迎來產品價量齊揚，尤其以高毛利的 Power 產品最為明
顯，根據客戶規劃，1.6T AEC 也將逐步起量，因此可預期 HPC 占比將
會再進一步提升，利於整體營運表現。綜合以上，研究部預估貿聯 2026
年 EPS 76.23 元，2027 年 EPS 131.41 元，目前 PE 為 11.6x，參考歷
史 PE 區間為 10-35x，考量貿聯獲利將維持高成長期，唯獨近期市場擔
憂資料中心建置速度恐受高利率環境而放緩，存在一定 AI 伺服器延宕部
屬之風險，因此研究部下修 PER 至 22x，目標價 2900 元，維持買進。

營收走勢圖

研究員/蔡佾璋 EASONTSAI@uni-psg.com

研究部維持上品（4770）買進之投資建議，主要考量：（1）半導體相
關產業進入百年一遇的擴產期，帶動相關廠商擴廠、擴線，以及對應
的運輸設備需求規劃大幅度提升，上品目前在手訂單由2026 年初的38
億提升至 80 億歷史新高，公司對於 2027 年營收展望由先前的+20%YoY
提升至+35-40%YoY。（2）2H25 至 3Q26 的 GM 反映前一年度景氣低迷
時的接單，隨著產業需求提升，訂單能見度已拉升至超過一年，公司
可擇優進行生產，未來一段時間公司 GM 將明顯改善，逐季往上提升至
單季 40%水準。（3）現有產能已達到滿載，2026 至 2028 年需求強勁
增長，以內襯設備為主的新廠將於 2026 年底開始貢獻營收，4Q26 至
1Q27 營運將出現顯著提升，再來將進入非常忙碌且全力拉升產能的時
期，2027 年彰濱廠產能可望+20%YoY，嘉興廠產能將年增 30%YoY，槽
車產能經擴充後，已由 2026 年初的約當 80 台提升至目前的 130-140
台，2027 年中更可望擴增至 180 台，公司對於未來展望看法非常樂觀。
研究部預估 2026、2027 年稅後 EPS 14.4 元、22.48 元(原估 12.88 元、
17.34 元)，分別+41.2%YOY、+56.1%YOY。考量目前回到半導體加速擴
廠循環，有利於上品營運回溫，過往 PER 評價區間落在 10-24 倍，目
前評價僅 10.5 倍，風險報酬比極佳，故維持上品買進之投資建議，目
標價 340 元（15xPER *2027EPS）。

重點摘要： 
1. 上品為氟素樹脂(鐵氟龍)生產商

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

上品為各項氟素樹脂(又稱鐵氟龍PFA)的生產商，提供塗層、
酸洗槽、管路系統等全方面鐵氟龍產品，主要用於高溫或腐
蝕性化學液之生產、儲存、純化、輸送等功能之設備及材料。
產能為台灣彰濱廠及中國浙江嘉興廠。

2. 上品內襯設備產品占比最高，另外也包括氟素樹脂材料及工
程收入 
上品的產品主要為因應半導體及各產業所需的氟素樹脂(包
括PTFE、M-PTFE、PFA、PFA-HP、PFA HP PLUS等產品)。產品
線包括氟素樹脂內襯板材與素板，內襯直管用管材，客製化
射出件與焊接材料等。

半導體製程中，所有進來的原料都必須避開金屬離子，需大
量使用氟素材料的純化系統(可避免溫度交換、反應或吸收)。
氟素樹脂內襯產品廣泛應用於半導體設備、生化科技，製藥
及精密化學業等高純度要求的槽車、桶槽和所有藥液接觸的
容器，以及客製化的熱交換器、溢流槽與治具。

圖 2、上品 3Q26 產業別營收比重

資料來源：公司、統一投顧整理

此外，上品的氟素工程收入主要是因應半導體及各產業客戶
所需，提供流體力學計算，高純化學品設備系統之設計規劃，
製造及安裝等一套完整服務。上品的競爭優勢為鐵氟龍下游
應用的研發能力強，產品自製率高達80-90%，可依據不同的材
料特性進行調整，客製化開發下游客戶所需的新產品。

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  3

在競爭格局上，上品為產業龍頭，且極具優勢，能跟公司競爭
的只有兩家日商，但這兩家日商的市佔率加總還不到公司的
一半。

3. 2026-2028年建廠需求顯著提升，在手訂單創歷史新高 
2H24開始，受到中國地區內捲競爭影響，拖累上品毛利率表現
從過往的45-50%區間下降至25-30%區間，中國地區營收占比
也逐漸下降。

圖 3、上品 3Q26 地區營收比重

資料來源：公司、統一投顧整理

目前來看，台灣地區占比持續擴大，且此趨勢將延續下去，主
因為主要客戶對中國供應鏈有所限制，公司未來主要將透過
台灣產能供應台灣、美國、日本、新加坡等中國以外市場。

毛利率表現取決於與客戶的接單狀況，2026前三季上品整體
毛利率已回升至35%, +0.8pctYoY。由於2025年市況不佳，所
以2H25至1H26出貨的訂單毛利率較低，然2H26年開始進入產
業復甦階段，目前市況已明顯比2025年樂觀。

### Page 4

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  4

圖 4、上品在手訂單創歷史新高

資料來源：公司、統一投顧整理

2026年初上品的在手訂單為38億元，但截至最新統計，在手訂
單已飆升至新台幣80億元。主要客戶的需求極為急迫，甚至
在半導體展期間派遣團隊到展會攤位確保供貨。

目前上品最核心的重點就是急速擴充產能，公司對2026年至
2028年的市場需求非常樂觀，2027年現有產線預計將全數塞
滿，必須持續擴產才能滿足客戶需求。

4. 百年一遇的半導體業擴廠熱潮 
上品近幾年成長動能皆來自於半導體業，由庫存儲備、運輸
及循環等需求同步帶動。公司觀察2026年為百年一遇的半導
體業擴廠熱潮。如台積電在論壇中所說，目前全球同時有近
20個廠在建，包括台灣本土13座，海外5-6座。在如此大規模
建廠下，半導體化學品的供應量完全不足，因而啟動了整個
供應鏈的建廠、擴產與運輸需求。尤其是先進封裝需要多種
類、小劑量的功能性化學品，自2025年起需求就非常快速成
長。預期2026年至2028年都將維持極強勁的需求。

上品對未來成長非常樂觀，目前的挑戰在於產能如何滿足客
戶的急迫訂單。這需要時間逐步擴增產能、培養技術人員及
拓展生產空間，同時也必須協調金屬加工下包供應鏈跟上步
伐，接下來將進入全力拉升產能的時期。

### Page 5

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  5

5. 預估4Q26稅後EPS 4.5元，+5.5%QoQ，+150.3%YoY 
上品2026 前三季營收39.6 億元，+7.8%YoY ，GM 35% ，
+0.8pctYoY，前三季累計GM轉為年增，而3Q26 GM呈現季減主
因為配合客戶出貨排程，3Q26認列了2025年景氣低谷期所承
接之較低毛利訂單，前三季累計營業利益9.1億元，+6.9%YoY，
稅後EPS 9.9元，+17.8%YoY。

10月營收4.9億元，-15.5%MoM，+79.3%YoY，營收達成率29.1%，
符合過往10月營收佔比平均約31%之預期，研究部預估上品
4Q26營收可望達16.8億元，+5.1%QoQ，+59.8%YoY，隨著營運
逐漸好轉，GM可望季增至36.9%，+2.3pctQoQ，+8.3pctYoY，
稅後EPS 4.5元，+5.5%QoQ，+150.3%YoY

6. 預估2026、2027年稅後EPS 14.4元、22.48元，分別+41.2%YoY、
+56.1%YoY 
展望2026年，全球半導體加速擴廠，上品目前在手訂單由2026
年初的38億迅速提升到80億歷史新高。研究部預估2026年營收
56.4億元(原估51.6億元)，+19.4%YoY，隨著營運逐漸好轉，GM
可望擴增至35.6%，+2.6pctYoY，稅後EPS 14.4元(原估12.88
元)，+41.2%YoY。

現有產線於預計將於2027年將全數塞滿，隨著新廠產能開出，
預計2027年整體營收將提升35-40%(原為年增20%)。研究部預估
2027年營收76.8億元(原估62.7億元)，+36.2%YoY，隨著營運逐
漸好轉，GM可望略增至39.5%，+3.9pctYoY，稅後EPS 22.48元
(原估17.34元)，+56.1%YoY。

## Document 240: [005930] 삼성전자(005930) 실적은 좋지만, 주주환원은 아쉽다삼성전자(005930) 실적은 좋지만, 주주환원은 아쉽다실적은 좋지만, 주주환원은 아쉽다 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005930] 삼성전자(005930) 실적은 좋지만, 주주환원은 아쉽다삼성전자(005930) 실적은 좋지만, 주주환원은 아쉽다실적은 좋지만, 주주환원은 아쉽다 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
아직 수요의 끝은 보이지 않는다 
AI 투자에 대한 우려의 목소리도 있지만 정작 부품을 공급하고 투자하는 쪽의 목소
리는 시간이 갈수록 더 커지고 있다. 공급 부족의 시점 매 분기 뒤로 밀려나고 있기 
때문이다. 28년까지 공급부족이라고 했던 메모리업체들은 이구동성으로 30년까지 공
급부족이 지속된다고 언급하고 있다. 이러한 변화는 실적에 고스란히 반영되고 있
다. 26년 3분기 메모리 영업이익만 108.5조원으로 추정한다. 4분기에는 140조원에 
이를 것으로 전망한다. 
HBM4와 QLC는 차별화 포인트 
삼성전자는 HBM에서 입지가 좋았던 적이 없었다. 점유율, 수익성, 가동률, 제품 성
능, 고객 등 모든 항목에서 경쟁사 대비 열위에 있었다. HBM4는 모든 항목에서 입
지를 개선시킬 변수가 될 것으로 예상한다. 26년 3분기 물량은 경쟁사 수준일 것으
로 보이고 연간 물량도 유사한 수준일 전망이다. 27년에는 가격 인상과 가동률 상승
으로 수익성도 크게 개선될 것으로 기대한다. QLC는 TLC와 함께 AI 모델 진화의 
수혜를 받을 제품으로 빠르게 부상하고 있다. 메타의 Muse 영향이다. 접속자당 가
상 메모리를 NAND에 100GB를 할당하고 있기 때문이다.  
부족한 주주환원으로 투자 우선 순위에서 밀릴 듯 
삼성전자에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. 이는 여러 변수에도 메모리 성장은 
변화가 없을 것으로 전망하고, 메모리 수요는 장기적 성장 국면에 진입했고, HBM 
시장 내 입지가 강화되고 있고, 현재 주가는 실적 대비 지나치게 저평가된 수준으로 
판단하기 때문이다. 목표주가는 350,000원으로 하향한다. 현재 수익성과 시장 상황
을 고려하면 적정 주가는 460,000원으로 판단하지만 주주환원 정책이 시장 기대치
에 미치지 못하고, 경쟁사와 비교해도 선호도가 낮아질 것을 고려하면 주가 상승은 
제한적일 것으로 판단하기 때문이다.

### Page 2

삼성전자 (005930)

2 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
26년 3분기 매출액은 195.3조원 
삼성전자의 26년 3분기 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 13.9% 증가한 195.3조원이다. VD/
가전을 제외한 주요 사업부 매출액은 2분기 대비 증가했다. DS, MX 모두 물량보다는 
가격 효과가 더 크게 작용한 것으로 분석된다. 사업부별로는  
1) DS 사업부 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 15.4% 증가한 147.1조원이다. 메모리
는 26년 2분기 대비 16.4% 증가했다. 26년 3분기 DRAM B/G(Bit Growth)
는 +2.0%, ASP는 +20.0%이다. NAND B/G는 +5.0%, ASP는 +18.0%이다. 
HBM 물량 증가는 HBM4가 주도했고, 그 이하 제품 비중은 하락한 것으로 
추정한다.  
2) 디스플레이 사업부 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 7.1% 증가한 8조원이다. 소형
은 해외 고객 및 국내 고객의 신제품 출시 영향으로 ASP 상승, 물량 증가 
효과가 동반되었고, 대형은 게이밍 모니터 비중을 확대했다. 
3) MX/네트워크 사업부 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 13.4% 증가한 37.7조원이
다. MX는 15.5% 증가했다. 물량은 소폭 감소했고, ASP 상승 영향을 받았다. 
네트워크 사업부 매출액은 2분기 대비 60% 감소했다.  
4) VD/가전 사업부 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 4.1% 감소한 13.9조원이다. VD 
매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 6.5% 감소했다. 가전은 26년 2분기 대비 1.3% 
감소했다.

그림 1. 삼성전자 사업부별 분기별 매출액 추이 및 전망

김운호  6915-5656

IBKS RESEARCH │3 
26년 3분기 영업이익은 107.4조원 
삼성전자의 26년 3분기 영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 20% 증가한 107.4조원이다. DS,  
디스플레이와 MX는 2분기 대비 증가했고, VD/가전은 2분기 대비 감소했다. 사업부별
로는 
1) DS 사업부는 26년 2분기 대비 19.4% 증가한 106.5조원이다. 2분기 대비 영
업이익률은 소폭 상승했다. 메모리 영업이익은 2분기 대비 19.0% 증가했고, 
비메모리는 영업적자를 유지했다.  
2) 디스플레이 사업부 영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 54.5% 증가한 1.1조원이다. 
중소형 신제품 효과로 수익성이 개선된 영향이다. 대형도 믹스 개선으로 수
익성이 개선되었다. 
3) MX/네트워크 사업부 영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 적자폭이 감소했다. 물량
은 줄었지만 High End 비중 상승으로 ASP는 개선되었고, 원가 비중이 낮아
진 영향이다. 
4) VD/가전 사업부 영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 적자 규모가 커졌다.

삼성전자 (005930)

6 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
26년 4분기 매출액은 226.5조원으로 예상 
삼성전자의 26년 4분기 매출액은 26년 3분기 대비 16.0% 증가한 226.5조원으로 예상
한다. DS가 증가하고 나머지는 부진할 것으로 예상한다.  
DS는 메모리가 성장을 주도할 것으로 기대한다. 가격과 물량 모두 3분기 대비 증가할 
것으로 전망한다. Context Server에 대한 수요가 증가하고, Personal Agent의 가상메
모리 설정으로 인해 NAND 수요가 증가하면서 QLC에 대한 수요 증가 속도가 빨라질 
전망이다. 이로 인해 NAND Bit Growth는 경쟁사와 차별화된 수준일 것으로 예상한
다.  
HBM 물량도 꾸준히 증가할 것으로 기대한다. 3분기에도 HBM4를 중심으로 물량이 
크게 증가했다. HBM4는 SK하이닉스와 비슷한 물량일 것으로 예상한다. 아직 전체 
물량은 SK하이닉스가 더 많은 상황이고, HBM 내 HBM4 비중은 업계 최고 수준인 것
으로 판단한다. 26년까지는 HBM이 수익성에는 마이너스 요인이지만, 27년에는 
DRAM 수준의 영업이익이 가능할 것으로 기대한다.  
MX는 비수기 저가 라인 물량 조정으로 전체 물량은 감소하지만, 제품믹스는 개선되
어 판가는 3분기 대비 상승할 것으로 예상한다.

26년 4분기 영업이익은 138.2조원으로 예상 
삼성전자의 26년 4분기 영업이익은 26년 3분기 대비 28.7% 증가한 138.2조원으로 예
상한다. DS 사업부를 제외하고 모두 3분기 대비 부진할 것으로 예상한다.  
DS 사업부 영업이익은 3분기 대비 30% 증가한 138.5조원으로 전망한다. DRAM, 
NAND 가격이 10% 초중반 수준으로 상승하고, 물량은 10% 중반 이상 증가하면서 3
분기 대비 영업이익률 개선을 기대하기 때문이다. HBM 물량 증가가 이익 개선에 미
치는 영향은 제한적일 것으로 예상한다.  
디스플레이, MX는 물량 부진으로 3분기 대비 영업이익은 부진할 것으로 전망한다.

DS가 성장 주도 
DS만 보자

### Page 7

김운호  6915-5656

IBKS RESEARCH │7 
Check Point 
1. 모자란 듯한 주주환원 
26년 3분기 실적 발표에서 밝힌 주주환원 정책은 지난 8월의 내용과 다르지 않다. 3
분기 배당 30조원(주당 4,601원)을 확정한 정도이다. 잔여 배당 재원이 55~75조원으
로 구체화했고, 현금 배당과 자사주 매입을 병행하겠다고 밝혔다.  
자사주 매입 시 우선주 매입도 고려 대상이라고 언급한 것은 고무적이지만 27년부터 
새롭게 시작되는 주주환원 정책을 결정하기 위한 이사회 개최는 27년 1월에 열릴 것
으로 예고하였기에 연내 추가적인 주주환원 발표는 없을 것으로 판단한다.  
2. HBM4로 HBM 시장에서 입지 강화 
HBM4를 통해서 자신감을 회복하고 있는 것으로 판단한다. 양산 시점은 경쟁사 대비 
빨랐고, 3분기 물량은 경쟁사와 비슷한 수준인 것으로 추정한다. HBM3e가 부진했기 
때문에 전체 생산량에서 HBM에서 HBM4 비중은 가장 높은 수준일 것으로 추정한다.  
26년 전체 물량도 경쟁사와 비슷한 수준이 될 것으로 전망한다. 삼성전자는 HBM 시
장 점유율을 DRAM 수준으로 맞추겠다는 목표를 3분기에 달성한 것으로 밝혔다.  
물량이 증가하면서 가동률도 빠르게 개선될 것으로 기대한다. 아직은 DRAM과 영업
이익률에서 큰 차이가 있지만 27년에는 인상된 가격, 높아진 수율, 가동률 개선으로 
DRAM과 영업이익률 차이를 좁힐 수 있을 것으로 기대한다.  
3. QLC NAND 시장 확대 
AI 컴퓨팅 확산은 NAND에게도 성장 기회이다. AI 모델들의 Context Window가 길어
져 KV Cache 메모리에 대한 수요가 커지면서 High Endurance TLC 수요가 급증하고 
있다. DRAM을 대체할 수준의 NAND가 필요하게 되었기 때문이다.  
이 뿐만 아니라 고용량 NAND에 대한 수요도 Personal Agent의 출현으로 급속히 증
가할 것으로 기대한다. 여기에는 QLC NAND가 주로 사용된다. 메타의 Muse는 접속
자 1인에 대해서 DRAM 8GB, NAND 100GB의 가상 메모리를 설정하고 운영한다.  
Agent AI의 확산은 스토리지에 대한 수요도 급격히 확대할 것으로 기대하고, 삼성전
자에게도 기회가 될 것으로 기대한다.  
26년 4분기 NAND Bit Growth는 삼성전자가 가장 높을 것으로 예상한다.

삼성전자 (005930)

8 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
투자의견 매수 유지, 목표주가 350,000원으로 하향 
삼성전자에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. 이는 
1) 여러 변수에도 메모리 성장은 변화가 없을 것으로 전망하고, 
2) 메모리 수요는 장기적 성장 국면에 진입했고, 
3) HBM 시장 내 입지가 강화되고 있고, 
4) 현재 주가는 실적 대비 지나치게 저평가된 수준으로 판단하기 때문이다. 
목표주가는 350,000원으로 하향한다. 현재 수익성과 시장 상황을 고려하면 적정 주가
는 460,000원으로 판단하지만 주주환원 정책이 시장 기대치에 미치지 못하고, 경쟁사
와 비교해도 선호도가 낮아질 것을 고려하면 주가 상승은 제한적일 것으로 판단하기 
때문이다.

## Document 256: [329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸HD현대중공업(329180) 3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸HD현대중공업(329180) 3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
악조건 속에서도 이 정도 
HD현대중공업의 2026년 3분기 영업실적은 매출액 5조 7,536억원(yoy +30.2%, qoq 
`-9.1%), 영업이익 8,992억원(yoy +61.3%, qoq -13.5%)으로 당사 추정치에 대체로 
부합하였다. 조업일수 감소와 원화 강세로 매출액이 감소했으며, 임단협 타결로 
인한 성과금 및 격려금이 당분기에 비용으로 반영되었다. 해양플랜트 부문에서는 
RUYA 프로젝트의 공사 진행률 확대와 TRION 프로젝트의 C/O(계약 변경) 
180억원이 추가로 반영되며 매출액이 전분기대비 0.7% 증가하였다. 엔진기계 
부문은 환율 하락을 판가 상승과 D/F(이중연료) 비중 증가로 상쇄하며 전분기와 
유사한 영업실적을 달성했다. 
2027년에도 지속될 수주 시점 효과 
3분기 대형선 야드의 수주 시점별 매출 비중은 2022년 4%, 2023년 45%, 2024년 
31%, 2025년 20% 수준으로 파악된다. 상대적으로 선가가 낮았던 2022~2023년 
물량이 여전히 매출의 절반 가량을 차지하고 있는 셈이다. 따라서 2027년 말까지도 
고선가 물량의 매출 비중 확대가 이어질 것으로 예상된다. 이는 LNG운반선 매출 
비중 감소에도 불구하고 수익성을 방어할 수 있는 핵심 요인으로 작용할 전망이다.   
투자의견 매수 유지, 목표주가 550,000원으로 하향 
투자의견은 매수로 유지하나, 목표주가는 550,000원으로 34.5% 하향한다. 환율 
가정을 기존 1,450원 수준에서 1,400원으로 조정함에 따라 목표주가 산정의 기준이 
되는 2027~2028년 실적추정치를 하향조정하였다. 또한 미국 네오 클라우드 
업체들의 자금 조달 여건 악화로 데이터센터 증설이 지연되고 있어, 발전용 엔진 
증설을 근거로 동사에 부여하였던 프리미엄을 제거하여 P/E 배수를 19.8배로 
하향조정했다. 그러나 본업인 상선 부문은 향후 고부가가치 가스선의 발주 강세를 
전망하기 때문에 투자의견은 매수로 유지한다. 
목표주가 (하향) 550,000원

## Document 271: Adobe预测：今年圣诞网购2750亿美元，借贷消费占213亿再创新高

Source PDF: all report pdf/Adobe预测：今年圣诞网购2750亿美元，借贷消费占213亿再创新高.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-27
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Adobe预测，11⽉⾄12⽉"先买后付"⽀出将达213亿美元，同⽐增⻓6.6%；其中82%通过
⼿机完成。整体假⽇在线总⽀出预计达2751亿美元，增⻓6.7%。与此同时，AI购物⼯具引
导流量预计激增130%，正深刻重塑消费者购物路径。
摘要：
美国消费者在今年假⽇购物季的借贷消费规模预计将创下历史新⾼，“先买后付”（BNPL）⼯具的
使⽤量持续攀升。
据Adobe预测，在11⽉1⽇⾄12⽉31⽇期间，美国消费者使⽤“先买后付”的在线借贷消费⽀出将
达到创纪录的213亿美元，较去年同期增⻓6.6%。
仅在“⽹购星期⼀”（Cyber Monday），该项⽀出预计将达到10.9亿美元，连续第⼆年突破10亿
美元⼤关；“⿊五”（Black Friday）也将额外贡献8.07亿美元的信贷消费。
这⼀⽀付模式的显著特征在于对智能⼿机的极度依赖。Adobe的数据显⽰，⾼达82%的“先买后
付”⽀出将通过⼿机完成，机构将这⼀现象直接归因于移动设备更容易触发消费者的“冲动购物”
⾏为。
上⼀个假⽇季，“先买后付”已推动了200亿美元的在线⽀出，增幅近10%。

这⼀创纪录的信贷消费规模，建⽴在整体假⽇在线⽀出稳步扩张的基础之上。在深度折扣的刺
激下，消费者的购物需求正呈现出新的特征，市场整体流量与购买转化率受到密切关注。
整体假⽇在线⽀出稳步扩张
“先买后付”数据的增⻓折射出更庞⼤的零售基数。Adobe预计，今年假⽇季美国在线总⽀出将达
到2751亿美元，同⽐增⻓6.7%。
其中，从感恩节到“⽹购星期⼀”的“⽹络周”（Cyber Week）五天时间内，预计将带来475亿美元
的销售额，同⽐增⻓7.4%，占整个假⽇季⽀出的17.3%。
从单⽇表现来看，“⽹购星期⼀”仍将是整个购物季中销售规模最⼤的⼀天，预计达151亿美元，
增幅6.2%；⽽“⿊五”的增速更为迅猛，预计增⻓9.2%⾄129亿美元。

此外，感恩节当天的在线销售额预计增⻓8.5%⾄69亿美元，其中63%的交易料将通过移动设备
完成，创下历史新⾼，⾼于去年同期的61.6%，也⾼于整个购物季57.4%的平均⽔平。
深度折扣推动⽇常必需品热销
整体销售额的增⻓部分得益于年内最⼤⼒度的价格让利。
Adobe指出，“⿊五”的强劲增⻓在⼀定程度上归功于电视、服装和家电等品类的极深折扣。“⽹
络周”期间的整体折扣⼒度预计最⾼可达标价的30%。
从促销节奏来看，电⼦产品和电脑在“⽹购星期⼀”降价幅度最⼤；电视、玩具、服装、家电和
家具在“⿊五”折扣最⼤；⽽体育⽤品的最佳买点则在感恩节。
引⼈关注的是，⼤量假⽇消费并未流向传统的节⽇礼物。消费者正利⽤折扣⼤幅囤积⽇常必需
品。与9⽉份的平均⽔平相⽐，“⽹络周”期间个⼈卫⽣⽤品的在线销量预计将激增150%，基础服

装飙升210%，婴⼉⽤品增⻓113%，宠物⽤品增⻓93%，家庭清洁⽤品增⻓49%。
信贷隐患与AI购物⼯具的崛起
随着“先买后付”在⽇常消费中的渗透，潜在的信贷压⼒正在显现。
据LendingTree去年的调查数据显⽰，⾼达四分之⼀的“先买后付”⽤⼾使⽤该贷款购买杂货，这
⼀⽐例⾼于前⼀年的14%；同时，41%的⽤⼾表⽰在过去⼀年中存在逾期还款记录，⾼于此前
的34%。
在⽀付⽅式演变的同时，AI正在深刻改变消费者的购物路径。
Adobe预计，今年假⽇季通过AI⼯具链接引导⾄美国零售⽹站的流量将飙升130%。其中，感恩
节的AI引导流量将⼤涨159%，“⿊五”和“⽹购星期⼀”也将分别上涨95%和88%。
在Adobe对5000名消费者的调查中，77%使⽤过AI购物的受访者表⽰对其购买决策感到更有信
⼼。
上述预测基于Adobe截⾄9⽉28⽇的数据模型，实际零售数据将在⻓周末期间陆续落地。作为参
考，去年假⽇购物季的实际在线销售额达到创纪录的2578亿美元，最终超出Adobe当时的预测
值44亿美元。

## Document 285: AI炒股新玩法！盯上3000万散户，提前24小时预测交易行为

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI炒股新玩法！盯上3000万散户，提前24小时预测交易行为.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

以⾊列AI公司42AI通过分析近3000万名投资者的交易⾏为，预测散⼾交易动向，帮助券商
优化对冲策略。与此同时，AI正从辅助散⼾构建交易策略，延伸⾄机构投研和⾃主投资。
AI降低了投资⻔槛，却也放⼤了机构的数据与资源优势，散⼾想要获取超额收益或将更加
困难。
摘要：
谁能想到，在⾦融市场上，AI最先研究透的可能不是市场，⽽是散⼾。
最近，以⾊列AI创业公司42AI正式结束隐⾝研发状态，并披露了6250万美元的种⼦轮融资。
这家公司的⽣意很有意思。过去⼏⼗年，华尔街投⼊了⼤量的⼈⼒和资⾦，试图预测⾦融资产
的涨跌。但42AI换了⼀个思路：既然⾏情这么难预测，能不能预测投资者的⾏为？
⽐如，⻩⾦突然⼤涨，哪些散⼾会忍不住追⾼，哪些散⼾会选择获利了结，⼜有多少⼈会加杠
杆？
42AI做的，就是把这些⾏为变成可以量化、预测的数据，再将预测结果卖给券商，帮助券商制
定对冲策略。
⽬前，42AI已经与10家⼤型券商展开合作，涉及近3000万名投资者的客⼾数据。据其创始⼈透
露，通过优化对冲策略，42AI已经为⼀家券商创造了约3000万美元的额外价值。

有意思的是，当⼀批AI公司开始帮机构研究散⼾时，另⼀批AI公司正在帮助散⼾提升投资能⼒。
⽐如，今年10⽉获得红杉资本投资的Catalyst，可以直接将普通投资者的想法转化为⾃动交易策
略。过去需要⼀个专业投资团队才能完成的策略开发、回测和⾃动交易，如今⼀个散⼾借助AI就
能完成。
听起来，AI似乎正在拉平散⼾与专业投资机构之间的能⼒差距。但研究了⼀圈AI在⾦融交易领域
的应⽤后，硅基君发现，事情可能没有那么乐观。
预测不了⾏情，那就预测散⼾
预测⾏情，可能是⾦融市场上最吃⼒不讨好的事情之⼀。
今年1⽉，⾼盛还在担⼼全球原油供应过剩，预计今年布伦特原油均价只有56美元/桶。结果美国
和伊朗打了起来，国际油价⼀度突破100美元/桶。
但如果换个思路呢？
虽然不知道明天油价是涨是跌，但如果油价突然上涨10%，投资者会做出什么反应，或许更容
易预测。毕竟，⾏情千变万化，但⼀个⼈的交易习惯、⻛险偏好往往有规律可循。
42AI就是顺着这个思路做起了⽣意。它瞄准了⼀类特殊的⾦融机构——CFD（差价合约）交易

商。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

这类券商主要为投资者提供⻩⾦、原油、外汇等资产的杠杆交易服务，赚钱的⽅式主要有两
种。
⼀种是赚交易费⽤。⽐如，客⼾做多⻩⾦，券商就在外部市场建⽴等额的多头仓位，将价格波
动的⻛险对冲掉，赚取交易费⽤与对冲成本之间的差额。
另⼀种则是直接与客⼾做对⼿盘。如果券商选择不对冲，那么客⼾亏100美元，券商就赚100美
元，反之亦然（暂不考虑交易费⽤）。
现实中，这类券商往往只会对冲部分交易⻛险，以争取更⼤的盈利空间。因此，哪些⻛险可以
⾃⼰承担，哪些⻛险必须对冲，直接关系到券商能赚多少钱。
42AI做的，就是帮券商把这笔账算得更准。
为此，42AI⼏乎将投资者与券商打交道的整个过程，都纳⼊了AI的研究范围。除了历史交易记
录、持仓、⼊⾦和出⾦记录外，就连客⼾给客服打了多少次电话、通话时的语⽓和情绪，都是AI
的分析对象。
⽐如，有些投资者喜欢在亏损后不断加仓，有些投资者稍微赚点钱就急着卖出，还有些投资者
⼀遇到剧烈⾏情就忍不住频繁交易。
42AI将这些⾏为转化为可量化的个⼈特征，再通过基于Transformer架构构建的⾏为世界模型，

预测投资者在不同市场环境下的反应。
据42AI披露，⽬前其系统最远可以提前24⼩时预测客⼾的交易品种、⽅向和交易量。
这些信息对券商有什么价值？假设⼀家券商的客⼾持有100份⻩⾦多单、80份空单，多空相互抵
销后，券商只需要在外部市场建⽴20份多单，就能对冲掉剩余的⻛险。
但如果接下来有40份多单被平仓，客⼾的整体持仓就会从净多头变成净空头。如果券商没能及
时调整仓位，原本⽤于降低⻛险的对冲交易，反⽽可能放⼤亏损。
42AI的价值，就是让券商在客⼾实际下单之前，就能对这些变化有所准备。
尤其是在极端⾏情下，这种能⼒相当值钱。
2025年10⽉21⽇，国际⾦价单⽇下跌约5%。据42AI创始⼈Amit Gal-Or透露，⼀家经营数⼗年
的⼤型CFD交易商，在这次⾏情中亏掉了5000万美元。
两个⽉后，这家券商找到了42AI。双⽅利⽤历史数据重新模拟了当天的交易情况，发现如果当
时采⽤这套系统，券商有望少亏数千万美元。
这笔账算完，双⽅很快便达成了合作。
⽬前，42AI除了向券商收取固定费⽤，还可以按照为券商创造的价值进⾏分成。

AI正在全⾯介⼊⾦融交易
42AI只是AI进⼊⾦融交易领域的⼀个缩影。
过去两年，AI在⾦融⾏业的应⽤已经开始从信息搜集、辅助投研，逐渐向投资决策和交易执⾏延
伸。据硅基观察不完全统计，⽬前全球已有12家代表性AI创业公司布局这⼀领域。

### Page 3

从商业模式来看，⼤致可以分为三类。
第⼀类，是帮助普通投资者构建交易策略。
⽐如，今年10⽉获得红杉资本投资的Catalyst。
假设你认为巴西⼲旱会导致咖啡减产，进⽽推⾼咖啡价格。
过去，对于⼀个普通投资者来说，即使这个判断是对的，也未必知道怎么赚钱。究竟应该买咖
啡期货，还是买相关公司的股票？什么时候建仓？买多少？判断错误后⼜该如何⽌损？
Catalyst可以帮助投资者进⼀步研究这些问题，将投资想法转化为具体的交易策略，并完成程序
编写、回测以及⾃动交易。
这⼤幅降低了普通投资者参与复杂⾦融交易的⻔槛。
第⼆类，是帮专业投资机构寻找交易机会。
与第⼀类不同，这类公司的客⼾主要是对冲基⾦等专业投资机构。
它们的核⼼价值在于扩⼤投资研究的⼴度，让机构能够同时探索更多市场、资产和投资策略，
从中寻找超额收益。
⽐如今年7⽉获得约500万美元融资的KelAI，可以接⼊基⾦内部的研究资料和私有数据库，让AI
⾃主提出投资假设、编写代码并进⾏回测。
即使策略回测失败，AI也会记录失败的原因。⽐如某个交易信号在美股有效，在其他市场却可能

失效。这些研究结果会被保留下来，成为AI下⼀轮研究的参考。
第三类，则是直接下场做投资。
今年4⽉，AI对冲基⾦Abundance披露获得了1亿美元的种⼦股权融资。
这家基⾦部署了数千个AI Agent，负责从互联⽹搜集信息、寻找投资机会、筛选做多和做空的股
票，到决定仓位⼤⼩、执⾏交易。⽬前，其部分选股策略已经完全由AI运⾏。
传统投资机构也在进⾏类似的尝试。2024年7⽉，全球知名对冲基⾦桥⽔⾯向外部客⼾推出了⼀
只由AI主导投资决策的基⾦。
桥⽔将过去数⼗年积累的投资案例交给AI，让其学习宏观经济事件与资产价格之间的因果关系。
⽐如，美联储缩表会如何影响债券价格，美国提⾼关税⼜会对不同资产产⽣什么影响。

### Page 4

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
211 收藏 分享到:
在这只基⾦中，数据获取、⻛险控制和交易执⾏仍然由⼈类主导，但究竟是做多⽇元还是做空
股票，已经由AI来决定。
截⾄今年9⽉底，这只基⾦的管理规模已经超过45亿美元，今年前三季度的收益率达到16.4%，
成⽴以来的年化收益率为13.2%。
从搭建交易策略，到寻找投资机会，再到直接管理资⾦，AI正在接⼿过去需要专业投资团队才能
完成的⼤量⼯作。
总结
听起来，AI似乎正在拉平散⼾与专业投资机构之间的能⼒差距。
过去，散⼾与专业机构之间的差距，很⼤程度上体现在信息获取和研究能⼒上。随着AI的普及，
这部分差距正在缩⼩。
但⾦融市场的特殊之处在于，投资能⼒的提升，并不意味着投资收益的提升。因为决定超额收
益的，是你相对于其他市场参与者的优势。
当所有⼈都能低成本地获取信息、分析财报、构建交易策略，这些能⼒本⾝的稀缺性也就下降
了。
真正值钱的，变成了AI背后那些⽆法被轻易复制的资源。⽐如，专业机构过去⼏⼗年积累的研究
笔记、专家访谈和交易经验，以及42AI能够接触到的近3000万名投资者的⾏为数据。
过去，受限于⼈⼒和技术，这些资源的价值远没有被充分挖掘。AI的出现，反⽽让机构有机会将
过去的资源积累转化为更⼤的竞争优势。
这可能才是AI时代散⼾⾯临的最⼤困境：⾃⼰的投资能⼒确实变强了，但对⼿变强的速度可能
更快。
AI固然⼤幅增加了散⼾的研究能⼒，却也让超额收益变得稀缺。
本⽂来源：硅基观察Pro

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
ECONOMIC INSIGHT
Tech stocks sold off in recent weeks, as the AI build-out’s
demand for resources and debt collided with uncertainty
about future revenues, and concerns about some founders’
judgement. Prices partially rebounded when Jensen
Huang was reinstated as NVIDIA CEO, but the uncertainty

remains. That uncertainty in turn stems from doubts over
the ultimate productivity benefit, and about who will
capture those gains. If AI doesn’t produce the hoped-for
wins, or if end-users reap most of the benefits, the labs’
revenue projections might be overly optimistic. Anthropic’s
document filing ahead of its IPO concentrated attention on
this question, though the scepticism and ‘bubble’ discourse
has been around for a couple of years already.

Nobody adopts a technology unless they are better off
doing so. At least some of the gains must therefore accrue to
the end-user firms and consumers or they will not adopt it.
Plenty of interesting technologies, such as blockchain or the
Metaverse, have not broken out of niches because they were
not solutions to problems many people had. Modern AI is
clearly not in that category, though: businesses and consumers
alike have adopted it rapidly, especially this year. But they will

only keep doing so if the cost makes the switch worthwhile.
The end-user must be better off, and this caps the revenue
capture available to the producer.
Another strand of scepticism about the technology downplays
the productivity benefits, at least those accruing in the next
couple of years. This scepticism is understandable. It takes time
to learn how to use any new technology effectively. A ‘J-curve’
can arise, where productivity initially dips before rising higher

than the pre-adoption level. The economics literature has
highlighted how J-curves can emerge, including for AI.
The learning phase for AI might be expected to be particularly
drawn out because of the so-called ‘jagged frontier’, where the
models are amazing at some tasks such as coding but poor at
others. The rapid evolution of the strongest models over the
past 12 months worsens this issue. Your intuition about what the
models can and should be used for must be continually updated.

In addition, the world is in the ‘Gin Lane’ phase of getting used to a
new product, with all the bad and antisocial consequences – cyber
risk, slop and fraud to name a few – arising ahead of the systems
to manage them. (And if the ‘Gin Lane’ metaphor holds, you might
expect AI ‘Temperance’ movements to emerge and move the
pendulum too far the other way before a balanced is reached.)
‘Double-you’ could well be true (but are we past the ‘J’?)
Luci Ellis

Chief Economist, Westpac Group
So far past Solow
If we grant that there will be a ‘J-curve’ on productivity from AI,
does that mean the benefits are still some years away? People
well recall the famous ‘Solow paradox’ that ‘computers are
everywhere except in the productivity statistics’. Likewise, there
was a multidecade lag between the introduction of electric
power and the productivity boost in manufacturing. We do not
think it is correct to extrapolate from those episodes, though.

There are reasons to expect at least some discernible effects on
economy-wide productivity measures within two years.
It comes down to timing. With AI adoption, many people start
the clock in 2022 with ChatGPT’s release, but that might be
the wrong starting point.
Consider that when Solow made his famous quip in 1987,
most personal computers still used clunky MS-DOS. Windows
3.0 was three years away still. Windows 95, the product that
mainstreamed easy-to-use interfaces, was eight years away.

Macs and laser printers existed and were revolutionising print
publishing, but given their price, did not break out of that niche for
many years. While it pains this long-time Mac user to admit, the
introduction of Windows 95 and dial-up internet was the ‘good
enough’ technology that accelerated growth in productivity.
The lag to that being visible in the data was just a few years.
Productivity growth had already picked up by the late 1990s.
We think that the introduction of LLMs and chat interfaces maps

more closely to the introduction of Windows 95, HTML and the
web, and retail internet connections in the mid-1990s than to
the advent of personal computing more than a decade earlier. On
that basis, the productivity benefits are likely to become evident
relatively quickly – perhaps not this year, but plausibly by 2028.
Like Windows 95 to computing, LLMs and their chat and
coding interfaces have democratised access to machine
learning models. Back in 2018, I expressed scepticism that

“There is a learning curve for any
new technology, but we could be
closer to seeing the economy-wide
productivity benefits than many
people think."

### Page 3

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
3
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
machine learning and AI would be widely adopted. Along
with its sister discipline ‘data science’, AI at the time was an
exercise in replacing relatively abundant skills – the bachelor-
degree or high-school level skills required of most white-collar

jobs – with the much scarcer PhD-level skills in mathematics,
statistics and computer science needed at the time to build
and tune these models. Replacing abundant skills with scarcer,
more expensive ones never works outside rare niches.
The LLM-with-harness revolution has changed my mind.
It has super-charged the already skilled – who would have
expected Navier–Stokes and the Riemann Hypothesis to both
be cracked in the same year? More importantly, though, it has

democratised access to ‘good enough’ versions of specialist
skills. There is a price to be paid for this advance in the form
of slop, but clearly a benefit as well.
There will be some learning curve still, and a blurring of
boundaries between jobs, just as the Mac and desktop
publishing got more people thinking about fonts and graphic
design, rather than delegate those choices to a specialist. The
end result will be a significant lift in productivity, and not in a

decade’s time but sooner.
This will not, however, necessarily mean model developers
make all the money. Unlike computer operating systems as a
platform for applications, AI models have no ‘moat’: there are
no network economics creating a ‘winner-take-more’ effect,
and no lock-in from an installed base. The interface means
there is little learning curve to switch between models. You
might need to check your output against the output from
the model you were previously using, but that is an issue

with new models from the same lab, too. Many users switch
around models easily and readily.
Waves of enthusiasm for the latest model from one or another
of the main labs, including in the past week, or a new open-
weights lab, demonstrates that the most engaged users of
these tools have little brand loyalty. The main barrier to entry
to this market is the setup costs of training a new model, but
there are already enough players, both at the frontier and the

fast-follower open-weights group, to keep competition intense.
Easy switching and willingness to do so add up to little pricing
power for producers. This is good news for economy-wide
productivity, not so much for long-term profitability and R&D
spend for independent model producers.
The productivity benefits of AI are coming, perhaps sooner
than some think. The result might not be literally ‘double-you’
exponential growth. A period of stronger global productivity

growth on the scale of the late 1990s would still be a much
better outcome for the global and Australian economies than
the past couple of decades.
No change to our RBA view
The October CPI data were a shade above our nowcast
(less than 0.1%pt), and we think there is upside risk to our
forecast for Q4 trimmed mean inflation.
As we highlighted in the preview note, though, we think
Q4 and October specifically were the peak. Nothing about
today’s data change that view. We are still forecasting a

negative print for headline CPI in November because of the
large fall in fuel prices. Trimmed mean is expected to moderate
next month as well. There were some upside surprises in
October in key components, including home-building costs,
which the RBA will be particularly sensitive to. However,
a number of components seen as bellwether indicators of
domestic inflation pressures or persistent inflation (rents,
dining out) actually came in a little below our forecasts.

We therefore do not see any implications for our RBA view
from this data at this early stage. The RBA is likely to remain
nervous about the possibility of entrenched inflation. Against
that, though, the labour market, housing market and real-
economy indicators are all breaking weaker than its November
forecasts. The Monetary Policy Board will be in something of
a holding pattern at next month’s meeting and will want to
confirm if Q4 really was the peak for inflation.

Further analysis from the team will be forthcoming later
today. For now, though, we see nothing to change our view
that the RBA is likely finished hiking rates but will still be
considering another move in coming months. Nor do we see
anything to change our view that the most likely date for the
first interest rate cut is August next year.

### Page 4

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
THE WEEK THAT WAS
Cliff Notes: in pursuit of future gain
That said, monthly PCE data suggests consumer momentum
waned at quarter end, with real spending up just 0.1% in
September and unchanged in October despite an acceleration
in nominal personal income growth (from 0.3% to 0.7%).

The Federal Reserve's Beige Book remains more downbeat than
the hard data. "Eight of the twelve Federal Reserve Districts
reported growth in the slight to moderate range, while four
Districts reported no change. Consumer spending was little
changed, as households remained price sensitive and were
increasingly selective in discretionary purchases." Given weak
population growth and uncertainty over momentum outside the
tech sector, employers are " focused on utilizing existing staff

more efficiently”, “with many firms reporting stable head counts
and hiring primarily to replace for attrition”. These findings
suggest that, should the pace of expansion in AI infrastructure
plateau or turn, US GDP could quickly return near trend.
The market’s mood continues to depend not only on the data
to hand but also ongoing threats to Middle East oil supply,
Houthi rebels struck Saudi assets again this week with limited
effect, and expectations for the AI buildout, which this week

was dominated by news around NVIDIA. US trade policy
also remains a threat, the Trump administration this week
announcing plans for a fresh round of tariffs on China, the
European Union and several other major trading partners
to replace previous levies struck down by the Supreme
Court. China’s 20% tariff could double to 40% should trade
negotiations break down. India may also face a 25% tariff if it
fails to reach a trade agreement with the US. Uncertainty is

likely to persist on each front well into 2027.
The main event in Australia this week was the October CPI
report which surprised modestly to the upside, headline
inflation rising 0.5% (4.9%yr). The main drivers were auto
fuel (+6.2%), domestic holiday travel (+4.5%) and new
dwelling costs (+0.8%). Fuelled by some project home
builders reducing discounts, the increase in new dwelling
costs was noteworthy; however, subdued demand should
see discounting return. More broadly, underlying inflation

continued to accelerate. Trimmed mean inflation gained
0.5% in the month and 3.8%yr, a cycle high that breaks the
3.5–3.6%yr range held since May. The six-month annualised
pulse was stronger still at 4.3%yr.
As noted by Westpac Chief Economist Luci Ellis following
the release, October likely marks the peak for inflation, but
the upside surprise in dwellings and other early-quarter
components skews the risks to our Q4 trimmed mean estimate
of 0.9%qtr up. The RBA is unlikely to relax its focus on price

risks until well into the new year.
This week, Australia also received two partial indicators for
investment ahead of next Wednesday’s Q3 GDP report.
Construction activity was in line with Westpac’s expectation,
up 0.9% in Q3 and 5.8% over the year. The detail shows
a clear rotation in activity: growth in infrastructure work
moderating (1.3%yr) as major public projects roll off; while
momentum in residential (9.3%yr) and non-residential
construction (10.2%yr) pick up. Further, a strong pipeline of

projects remains in place, ready to soak up any additional
capacity released by public infrastructure completions.
Private CAPEX subsequently surprised to the upside, up
12.2% in Q3, surpassing Westpac’s top-of-the-market forecast
of 10%. The headline result was driven by another very strong
showing for data centre-related investment, represented
by a 184% surge in machinery and equipment spending in
information media and telecommunications. There are also

signs of a nascent uptrend in investment forming across other
sectors, including utilities, financial and insurance services, and
retail trade. At 16%yr, real (inflation-adjusted) capex plans for
2026-27 are the strongest since the mining investment boom.
Ahead of next Wednesday’s release, our Q3 GDP preview will
be published later today on Westpac IQ.
Offshore this week, the data released corroborated known
themes. Ahead of Thanksgiving, US Q3 GDP growth was

revised up from 3.4% annualised to 4.3% in the second
estimate. Underlying the result was stronger growth in
personal consumption, revised up from 3.8% annualised to
4.2%, and a broad-based upgrade for business investment.
Elliot Clarke, Head of International Economics
Ryan Wells, Economist
Illiana Jain, Economist
"Australia’s economy continues to operate
near capacity as rapid growth in technology
investment offsets the winding down of public
infrastructure projects."

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
FOCUS ON NEW ZEALAND
Edging closer to neutral
We expect the RBNZ to deliver a final OCR hike for 2026,
lifting the OCR to 3%. A split vote seems on the cards,
especially if financial markets remain as volatile as they
have been in recent weeks. The MPC is likely to remain

resolved to return the OCR to a neutral setting, which they
see in a broad 3-3.5% range, with differences of opinion
centred on the timing of increases rather than the general
direction of interest rates.
The MPC has coalesced into hawkish and dovish factions.
The hawks (Conway, Silk and Gourley) are likely to remain
concerned about the inflation outlook and the still stimulatory
level of the OCR. This is Silk’s final meeting before her
departure from the RBNZ, with a replacement for the new

Assistant Governor Monetary Policy role expected to be
appointed sometime in the new year. The doves (Breman, Gai,
Hansen and Williams) likely still agree that further tightening
is required, but may see scope to move gradually given global
uncertainties, high unemployment (and consequently low
prospects of wage-driven inflation) and the potential for lower
energy prices should Middle Eastern tensions ease. Given the
finely balanced 3:4 hold decision in October, it is plausible some

doves will favour extending the pause, so a vote seems likely.
Even so, we think the case to hike to 3% will win the day. The
MPC has been resolved to lift the OCR to 3% for most of the last
six months, 3% sits at the low end of neutral estimates implied
by the RBNZ’s projections, and the MPC has previously been
unanimous that 3.25% is a reasonable target over time. For most,
a move to 3% should be seen as low risk and uncontroversial.
More interest will be in the RBNZ’s 2027 projections. The

September forecasts implied one further increase to 3.25%
by late 2028. We expect a modest upgrade of perhaps 10-20
bp, bringing the next 25bp increase forward to mid-2027, with
perhaps a 50% probability of a hike in February or March 2027
and a smaller probability of a move to 3.5% after that. This
would see the Q1 2027 average OCR around 3.05%, rising to
around 3.35% in Q2. Such a profile would be a shot over the
bows of the markets to discourage “end of cycle” type trades,

while providing plenty of flexibility to tailor the timing of the
next increase to the data flow.
The RBNZ’s read on the weaker exchange rate and labour
market will also be of especial interest. If it views the weaker
exchange rate as transitory, a more dovish message could
result. Its output gap-based framework tends to downplay
unemployment in driving medium term inflation, but should the
MPC place more weight on elevated unemployment, the RBNZ

may have time to play with before lifting the OCR much further.
We see three alternative scenarios for this meeting. In a
hawkish scenario (about 10% probability), the RBNZ lifts the
OCR to 3% but raises the projected track enough to imply a
greater than 50% chance of 3.75%, most likely if it is more
concerned about the inflation impact of the weaker exchange
rate or a higher neutral estimate. In a dovish scenario (about
20%), the OCR rises to 3% with the profile unchanged, leaving

the next increase some way off, as would occur if the MPC is
confident the cycle is nearly over and inflation will dissipate.
In a mega-dovish scenario (about 10%), the OCR stays at
2.75% with a broadly unchanged profile, likely if the MPC is
very concerned that global shifts (the Australian outlook, AI or
geopolitical troubles) could undermine the recovery.
Finally, the official result of the 2026 General Election has just
been announced. The results showed a swing back towards the

centre-left, but not by enough to allow the centre-left a realistic
path to power. The Centre-right grouping of National, NZ First
and ACT secured 65 seats in a 130-seat parliament. These results
imply the centre-right will need the support of The Opportunity
Party to return to government. The size of the National Party
overhang was reduced by 2 seats reflecting the overturning of the
narrow leads held in Hamilton East (Labour won by 173 votes)
and Wairarapa (Labour won by 81 votes). These narrow margins

raise the prospect of further delays in the negotiating process as
recounts may be requests. Such recounts don’t seem likely to shift
the results based on historical experience.
A tough period of negotiations between the centre-right
parties and TOP lies ahead. TOP has indicated it will reach out
to the National Party to begin discussions. However, as the
agreement of all three parties on the right will be required, the
likelihood is it will be the New Year before a new agreement

is forged. Uncertainty may plague markets for a while even
though the initial take of the market was for a small rise in the
NZD and short-term interest rates.
Kelly Eckhold
Chief Economist NZ

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
AUS: Q3 Inventories (%qtr)
Nov 30, Last: –0.2, Westpac f/c: –0.5
Market f/c: -0.3, Range: -1.6 to 0.5
Private non-farm inventories fell 0.2% in Q2, driven by a
rundown in mining inventories, unwinding a build-up from Q1
which reflected weather-related port closures. Consumer-

facing industries recorded inventory gains, including retail
trade, wholesale trade, and accommodation and food services.
For Q3, we expect private non-farm inventories to decline
0.5%, detracting 0.1ppt from GDP for the quarter. Non-mining
businesses likely drew down inventories to meet demand
amid subdued imports of consumer and intermediate goods.
Consumer goods imports increased by just 1.5%qtr, while
we estimate imports of intermediate goods, including fuels,

declined by almost 7%qtr, suggesting businesses relied more
heavily on existing inventories. This is expected to be partly
offset by a modest rebuild in mining inventories.
AUS: Oct Household Spending Indicator (%mth)
Nov 4, Last: 0.6, Westpac f/c: 0.5
Mkt f/c: 0.3, Range: -0.2 to 0.7
The monthly household spending indicator rose 0.6%mth in
September, following a slight 0.1%mth contraction in August,
annual growth lifting to 6.8%yr.
Our Westpac-DataX Card Tracker showed a solid rise in the

October month, centred on a significant price-driven increase
in fuel. New vehicle sales (which are not covered by the tracker
but are in scope for the spending indicator) look to have posted
a rise as well. Overall we expect the ABS indicator to show a
0.5% rise in the October month.
AUS: Oct Private Sector Credit (%mth)
Nov 30, Last: 0.6, Westpac f/c: 0.5
Market f/c: 0.5, Range: 0.4 to 0.6
Private sector credit growth was slightly firmer than expected

at 0.6% in September, leaving credit growth running at 8.4%yr.
Housing credit growth is slowing unevenly – concentrated on
the investor segment, with the tax changes for investment
properties announced in the May budget a clear driver. While
the owner-occupier segment has been relatively resilient to
date, some broadening of the slowdown is likely over the period
ahead. Business credit meanwhile remains a source of strength.
For October, we have pencilled in a lift of 0.5%. We expect to

continue seeing weakness in investor credit, and some hints of
softening owner-occupier and business credit over the coming
months.

AUS: Q3 Company Profits (%qtr)
Nov 30, Last: 1.8, Westpac f/c: 1.6 
Market f/c: 1.4, Range: 0.0 to 4.0
Headline company profits grew 1.8% in the June quarter. Mining 
profits rebounded 6.8%qtr, partially recovering the 9.4%qtr 
decline recorded in Q1. Outside mining, non-mining profits 
declined 1.1%qtr but remained a solid 7.7% higher over the year.
We expect company profits to grow 1.6%qtr in Q3. Mining profits 
are likely to recover strongly, supported by the large increase 
in mining-related export values, particularly LNG. By contrast, 
non-mining profit growth is expected to improve modestly as the 
Middle East-related fuel price shock fades and demand recoverng 
over the quarter. This is likely to be partly offset by higher award 
wages, which weighed on margins in consumer-facing sectors 
with relatively high award-wage coverage.
AUS: Nov Cotality Home Value Index (%mth)
Dec 1, Last: –1.2, Westpac f/c: –1.8
The Cotality home value index – covering all of Australia – 
fell 1.2% in October following a 1.3% decline in September 
and 1.4% falls in August and July. Annual growth has turned 
negative, running at –2.8%yr with prices down 6.7% from 
their peak in March. Brisbane and Perth led the declines in 
the October month but the correction to date remains more 
pronounced in Sydney and Melbourne.
The November update is set to be significantly weaker with 
another RBA rate rise in the mix and the daily measure 
(covering the five major capital cities rather than all of 
Australia) tracking a 2% decline vs 1.5% in October. The detail 
points to large monthly falls in Brisbane (–3%), Perth (–2.5%) 
and Sydney (–2.1%). We expect the national measure to show 
a 1.8% decline, a touch milder. Downward revisions, which 
have been pronounced this year, are also a risk.
AUS: Sep Dwelling Approvals (%mth)
Nov 2, Last: 5.1, Westpac f/c: –10.0 
Market f/c: -3.6, Range: -10.0 to 0.0
Approvals rose 5.1% in September but with the detail 
suggesting the underlying cycle is starting to turn down. 
The headline came from a big rise in the volatile private units 
component (+16.6%mth) which was partially offset by a 
sizeable decline in private detached houses (–5.3%mth).

We expect the October update to show a much clearer 
weakening. While it does not map tidily to monthly approvals, 
the HIA measure of new home sales has weakened 
dramatically over the last six months, dropping ~40% 
between April and October. With unit approvals coming off a 
lumpy gain, both units and detached house approvals could 
record large monthly declines. On balance we expect total 
approvals to drop 10% but it would not take much to get a 
more significant double-digit fall.

### Page 8

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.8
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
AUS: Q3 Net Exports (ppt cont’n to GDP)
Dec 1, Last: 0.1, Westpac f/c: –0.8
Market f/c: -0.3, Range: -0.8 to 0.2
Net exports look to have detracted from GDP growth in Q3;
we expect a contribution of –0.8ppts.
Another surge in automatic data processing (ADP) equipment

imports is going to be the main driver of the detraction. The
$11.1bn increase in these ADP equipment imports during Q3
will be largely volume-driven, similar to Q1, which also saw
net exports detract significantly from GDP.
While there have been some offsetting factors in the nominal
trade detail – strong LNG exports, a pull-back in fuel imports
– these look to have been largely price-driven, and hence will
have a smaller impact on net export volumes.

AUS: Q3 GDP (%qtr)
Dec 2, Last: 0.4, Westpac f/c: 0.5
Market f/c: 0.5, Range: 0.3 to 0.7
We expect the Q3 2026 National Accounts to show that
Australia’s economy expanded 0.5% (2.3%yr). Labour productivity
should record its strongest year-ended pace in almost a decade.
Despite significant headwinds, including higher fuel prices
and interest rates, the economy remains resilient. This is
partly due to a significant tailwind coming from a business
investment surge centred on data centres, renewables and the

infrastructure required to roll-out the AI revolution. Growth in
Q3 should also be supported by gains in housing construction,
household consumption and public demand.
For more detail, see our full preview.
AUS: Q3 Current Account Balance ($bn)
Dec 1, Last: –27.2, Westpac f/c: –25.5
Market f/c: -25.0, Range: -31.0 to -24.0
Q3 is shaping up to be another quarter with several competing
forces. Goods exports increased strongly, especially LNG
exports, although this was mostly price-led. On the import

side, there was another surge in ADP equipment imports –
capturing the investment upswing in data centres – although
this partly looks to be offset by a pull-back in fuel imports.
Services trade will likely be more of a drag – falling
international student commencements may hamper education
exports, while outbound tourism spending is set to rebound
following the normalisation of international flights at the onset
of the Middle East conflict.
All up, we have pencilled in a narrowing of the current account

deficit to $25.5bn.

### Page 9

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.9
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
NZ: Nov ANZ Business Confidence (index)
Nov 30, Last: 52.5
Firms remained optimistic in the October survey of business
confidence. Most notably, the resurgence in fuel prices over
September and October didn’t appear to faze them in the way
that the initial oil price shock did, suggesting that businesses

are simply learning to live with higher costs. The cost and
pricing measures of the survey will be of particular interest
ahead of the RBNZ’s 9 December policy statement, given the
policy committee’s seemingly diverging views on the extent of
second-round and indirect inflation pressures.
NZ: Oct Monthly Employment Indicator (%mth)
Nov 30, Last: 0.3, Westpac f/c: 0.3
The MEI has shown a gradual acceleration in jobs growth
in recent months, after a flat patch through the first half of

this year. The weekly snapshots provided by Stats NZ show
a further pickup over October, though this is most likely
related to the temporary hiring of election workers. While this
presents some upside risk to our estimate of a 0.3% increase
for the month, we suspect that this effect will show up more
fully in the November figures.
AUS: Oct Goods Trade Balance ($bn)
Dec 3, Last: 2.8, Westpac f/c: 1.0
Market f/c: 1.5, Range: 1.0 to 2.7
Goods trade surprised materially to the upside in September,

recording a surplus of $2.8bn. As has been the case for some,
ongoing volatility in trade of non-monetary gold – both on the
import and export side – has contributed to significant swings
in the headline goods trade balance.
Importantly, Sep’s surplus was despite the back-to-back mega
print for automatic data processing equipment imports – which
capture the investment upswing related to data centres.
For Oct, we have pencilled in a surplus of $1.0bn. Port data

suggests a lift in volumes for some resource exports and
commodity prices have been steadier in AUD terms, but
volatility in gold and ADP equipment imports will likely
continue to dominate.

### Page 10

Nonfarm payrolls surprised to the upside in October with a 
165k increase in the month and 84k jobs added to August and 
September. As a result, the 3-month average lifted to 137k, 
ahead of estimates consistent with balance between labour 
demand and supply.
November is likely to see a more modest gain and the prior two 
months are susceptible to a downward revision. Still, a gain 
of around 100k, expected by Westpac and the market, is best 
characterised as robust if not strong given current population 
growth. Ongoing resilience in labour demand is likely to see a 
higher level of participation in coming months, which is behind 
our unemployment rate forecast of 4.2%.
NZ: Oct Building Consents (%mth)
Dec 2, Last: -7.9, Westpac f/c: -2.0
Dwelling consent issuance remains firm, with just over 41,000 
new dwellings consented in the year to September. However, 
it looks like a peak may have been reached, with the number of 
medium-density developments easing back in recent months. 
We expect that pattern will have continued in October and 
are forecasting a 2% fall.  In the non-residential space, we’re 
continuing to see softness in the number of retail and office 
developments, but greater resilience in the industrial and 
storage segments.
